The handler surface is the C headers `abi/context.h`, `abi/core.h`, `abi/thread.h`, `abi/handler.h` (M2) and `abi/ticket.h` (M3), the processor and engine descriptors of `abi/lifecycle.h`, `abi/processor.h` and `abi/engine.h` (section 7), and the job-options setters of `abi/config.h`; `anira.hpp` is the C++ spelling over them and adds nothing the C header cannot do. `abi/context.h` is section 4's block; the handler surface starts here, at the inference threads a host drives itself, which exist only when the context was configured with `num_threads == 0` (section 4); on WebAssembly the context's own pool runs as well, as Wasm Workers (section 6b). Export macro, error model, ownership, enumeration convention and the thread-tag vocabulary are stated once in section 6a and assumed here; in the blocks below `[tag]` is the thread tag, `[cs]` = `[callback-safe]`, `NB` = `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` after the declarator, and the `ANIRA_API`/`ANIRA_CALL` pair and the `ANIRA_NOEXCEPT` every declaration carries are elided for readability and present in the real header.

```c
/* ================= abi/enums.h — values pinned, independent of build options ============== */
typedef enum anira_ticket_status { ANIRA_TICKET_PENDING = 0, ANIRA_TICKET_MET = 1, ANIRA_TICKET_LATE = 2, ANIRA_TICKET_DROPPED = 3,
                                   ANIRA_TICKET_FAILED = 4, ANIRA_TICKET_STATUS_FORCE32 = 0x7fffffff } anira_ticket_status;
typedef enum anira_pad_policy   { ANIRA_PAD_REJECT = 0, ANIRA_PAD_ZEROS = 1, ANIRA_PAD_POLICY_FORCE32 = 0x7fffffff } anira_pad_policy;

/* ================= abi/thread.h — user-driven inference threads; a Wasm Worker on WebAssembly ===== */
anira_status anira_inference_thread_create(anira_context*, anira_inference_thread** out, anira_error* err);   /* [main-thread]; bound to the core's queue, outlives the context; the handle type is abi/status.h's */
void         anira_inference_thread_run_loop(anira_inference_thread*);    /* [inference-thread]; blocks until stop; sets has_exited on return */
anira_bool   anira_inference_thread_execute(anira_inference_thread*);     /* [inference-thread]; dequeue + dispatch, allocation-free; the engine call and host callbacks may allocate and block */
anira_status anira_inference_thread_start(anira_inference_thread*, anira_error* err);   /* [main-thread]; native: an OS thread at the inference-thread scheduling class (real-time where granted); Wasm: a Wasm Worker on the module's memory that imports the context's worker module first; INVALID_STATE while running, OUT_OF_MEMORY when the OS or the browser refuses the thread */
void         anira_inference_thread_stop(anira_inference_thread*);        /* [main-thread]; native: joins; Wasm: waits for the Worker to leave its loop, then terminates it (a Worker still booting has its boot cancelled); a loop the host runs itself is asked to return only */
anira_bool   anira_inference_thread_has_exited(const anira_inference_thread*) NB;    /* [thread-safe]; true once run_loop returned (shared-memory atomic); required before destroy on Wasm */
anira_bool   anira_inference_thread_should_exit(const anira_inference_thread*) NB;   /* [thread-safe] */
anira_bool   anira_inference_thread_is_running(const anira_inference_thread*) NB;    /* [thread-safe] */
void         anira_inference_thread_destroy(anira_inference_thread*);     /* [main-thread]; native: stops and joins first; Wasm: refused with one Error record unless has_exited */
uint32_t     anira_num_inference_threads(void);                           /* [thread-safe]; the size of the default pool in this copy: 0 before the first handler and when the context brought its own threads */

/* ================= abi/handler.h — the handle types are abi/status.h's ======================== */
anira_status anira_pipeline_create(anira_pipeline** out, anira_error* err);   /* one inference stage, at most one processor, the custom engines */
anira_status anira_pipeline_add_inference(anira_pipeline*, const anira_model_config* const* variants, uint32_t num_variants,
                                          const anira_backend_id* candidates, uint32_t num_candidates, anira_error* err);   /* one variant in this pre-release; NULL/0 candidates = the default set: every engine this build carries on ANIRA_PROVIDER_CPU, every custom engine an entry names, every provider an entry is pinned to */
anira_status anira_pipeline_add_processor(anira_pipeline*, const anira_processor_desc*, anira_error* err);   /* copied into a refcounted carrier; at most one per pipeline, a second call is ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_STATE */
anira_status anira_custom_engine_create(const char* engine_id, const anira_engine_desc*, anira_custom_engine** out, anira_error* err);   /* the engine object: id and descriptor copied into a refcounted object, the identity the pool shares loaded models by; the id is reverse-URI, the prefix "anira." is anira's own ("anira.v2.custom" excepted) */
void         anira_custom_engine_destroy(anira_custom_engine*);   /* drops the handle's reference; pipelines, handlers and loaded models keep theirs */
void         anira_custom_engine_detach(anira_custom_engine*);    /* the handle stops keeping the engine alive and names it only while something else holds it (anira.hpp's Engine) */
anira_status anira_pipeline_add_engine(anira_pipeline*, const anira_custom_engine*, anira_error* err);   /* under the id the engine was created with: one more implementation the model entries name by id, not a processor, legal before or after anira_pipeline_add_inference; an id twice in one pipeline is ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_STATE */
anira_status anira_pipeline_capabilities_backends(const anira_pipeline*, const anira_context*, uint32_t element_size, uint32_t* count, anira_backend_id* out);   /* the context's rows, then every custom engine's providers usable here (its query runs) */
anira_status anira_pipeline_capabilities_edge(const anira_pipeline*, const anira_context*, anira_domain from, const anira_backend_id* to, anira_edge_info* out);
void         anira_pipeline_destroy(anira_pipeline*);
anira_status anira_handler_create(anira_context*, const anira_pipeline*, anira_handler** out, anira_error* err);   /* [main-thread]; copies everything, add-refs the context; checks the variant's structure, the named candidates against this build and every plan's provider against its engine; the models load at prepare in this pre-release */
void         anira_handler_destroy(anira_handler*);           /* [main-thread & !loader-lock]; releases the session (in-flight inferences drain, the pool joins with the last session of this copy), calls the processor's unprepare, drops the context reference and the processor and engine carriers */
anira_status anira_handler_prepare(anira_handler*, const anira_contract*, anira_error* err);   /* [main-thread & !processing]; blocking quiescence point; one plan per model entry and candidate that names its engine and a provider it accepts, dense-indexed; loads, warms up, sizes the rings, selects the default engine's plan (else plan 0), last calls the processor's prepare(info, user_data, &prepared) */
const anira_plan_report* anira_handler_plan_report(const anira_handler*);   /* [main-thread & prepared]; valid until next prepare/destroy */
uint32_t     anira_handler_num_entries(const anira_handler*);   /* [main-thread & prepared]; the chunks in flight at once: the size of a processor's per-chunk scratch, indexed by anira_processor_ctx.entry */
typedef struct anira_plan_slot { uint32_t struct_size; uint32_t slot; uint32_t is_input; uint32_t domain_in; uint32_t domain_out; uint32_t edge_class;
                                 uint32_t allocate_class; uint32_t wait_strategy; const char* recipe; const char* reason; uint32_t role; uint32_t binding; } anira_plan_slot;
                                 /* role: the spec's anira_role, which decides the entries that take the slot; binding: ANIRA_BINDING_NAME, _POSITION or _ENGINE (a custom engine bound itself) */
typedef struct anira_plan_ext  { uint32_t struct_size; uint32_t index; const char* host; const char* kind; const char* consumer; } anira_plan_ext;   /* consumer: "processor", a built-in engine's word ("libtorch") or a custom engine's id */
typedef struct anira_plan_info { uint32_t struct_size; uint32_t variant; uint32_t engine; uint32_t provider; const char* engine_id; double budget_ms;
                                 uint32_t engine_flags; uint32_t reserved; const char* provider_id; } anira_plan_info;
                                 /* engine_id: NULL for a built-in engine, the custom engine's id beside ANIRA_ENGINE_CUSTOM; provider_id likewise beside ANIRA_PROVIDER_CUSTOM;
                                    engine_flags: a custom engine's ANIRA_ENGINE_FLAG_*; budget_ms is the budget of that one plan (the contract's explicit figure in this
                                    pre-release), and the contract's Hard promise is the worst case across every plan (section 7) */
#define ANIRA_VARIANT_ANY 0xffffffffu
typedef struct anira_plan_query { uint32_t struct_size; uint32_t variant; anira_backend_id backend; } anira_plan_query;
                                 /* ANIRA_VARIANT_ANY, ANIRA_ENGINE_NONE and ANIRA_PROVIDER_NONE match every plan, and so does ANIRA_PLAN_QUERY_INIT */
/* A plan is addressed by a dense index 0..num_plans-1, never by a composite key: a dense index cannot name a plan
   that does not exist, enumerating the set is one loop, a later dimension (batch, device) appends a field to the row
   instead of moving every signature, and the index the report hands out is the same index selection takes. */
uint32_t     anira_plan_report_num_plans(const anira_plan_report*);
anira_status anira_plan_report_plans(const anira_plan_report*, uint32_t element_size, uint32_t* count, anira_plan_info* out);
anira_status anira_plan_report_slots(const anira_plan_report*, uint32_t plan, anira_bool inputs, uint32_t element_size, uint32_t* count, anira_plan_slot* out);
anira_status anira_plan_report_exts (const anira_plan_report*, uint32_t plan, uint32_t element_size, uint32_t* count, anira_plan_ext* out);
anira_status anira_plan_report_find (const anira_plan_report*, const anira_plan_query*, uint32_t* plan) NB;   /* [thread-safe]; the first plan in dense order that has every property the query names; ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_FOUND, plan untouched, when none */
/* ---- runtime selection: one relaxed atomic store, never planning ---- */
anira_status anira_handler_set_plan(anira_handler*, uint32_t plan) NB;   /* [thread-safe]; the dense index the report hands out; relaxed store (SessionElement.cpp:71-75), effective at the next submitted chunk; keeps the declared state: every plan of the one variant runs the same State pairs; out of range: a no-op, ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG */
uint32_t     anira_handler_get_plan(const anira_handler*) NB;            /* [thread-safe] */
/* ---- Hard entries: [driver-thread] NB unless noted; one const anira_tensor per slot, HOST or HOST_PINNED, rank 2 [channels, samples], planar
        (ANIRA_TENSOR_PLANAR), read by strides or packed, in the slot's ring dtype (nothing converts); a descriptor is never written, the memory
        is borrowed until the call returns; ANIRA_OK, ANIRA_MISSED (the miss policy filled the block: a success) or a failure recorded in rt_error;
        delivered is a nullable pure out-parameter, written on every return ---- */
anira_status anira_handler_process(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* in, uint32_t in_slot, const anira_tensor* out, uint32_t out_slot, size_t* delivered) NB;
anira_status anira_handler_process_multi(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* inputs, uint32_t num_inputs, const anira_tensor* outputs, uint32_t num_outputs, size_t* delivered) NB;
             /* one tensor per slot of the model's two lists: a Streamed slot's block, a Static slot's whole tensor, the empty tensor for a State slot or a slot left out; num_outputs counts */
anira_status anira_handler_push_data(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* in, uint32_t slot) NB;
anira_status anira_handler_push_data_multi(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* inputs, uint32_t num_inputs) NB;
anira_status anira_handler_pop_data(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* out, uint32_t slot, size_t* delivered) NB;
anira_status anira_handler_pop_data_multi(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* outputs, uint32_t num_outputs, size_t* delivered) NB;
anira_status anira_handler_set_static_input(anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, const anira_tensor*) NB;       /* the whole Static tensor in the spec's shape and dtype, copied under the slot's latch; legal from create on, prepared or not */
anira_status anira_handler_get_static_output(anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, const anira_tensor* out) NB;  /* the value the latest collected inference produced, copied under the latch, never torn; non-const: a refusal is recorded */
uint32_t     anira_handler_get_latency(const anira_handler*, uint32_t slot) NB;      /* [thread-safe]; valid from prepare on; the 2.x arithmetic incl. the wait_ratio credit, or the contract's declared figure; 0 for a Static or State output, index-aligned */
anira_status anira_handler_get_latencies(const anira_handler*, uint32_t* count, uint32_t* out) NB;   /* [thread-safe]; valid from prepare on; one entry per output slot */
anira_status anira_handler_get_available_samples(anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, uint32_t channel, size_t* out) NB;   /* [driver-thread]; collects the completed inferences and so runs post_process (InferenceManager.cpp:738-747) */
void         anira_handler_reset(anira_handler*) NB;           /* [driver-thread]; wait-free; in-flight results discarded, the HOLD_LAST block dropped, rt_error cleared; declared state re-zeroed on the inference thread at the new stream's first inference, engine and processor state through their reset slots */
anira_status anira_handler_rt_error(const anira_handler*) NB;  /* [thread-safe] [cs]; last-wins, relaxed atomic: a contract violation, ENGINE after a failed inference, CAPACITY after a dropped hop; ANIRA_OK if none */
/* ---- _wait twins: the bare form with double timeout_ms appended; [any-thread, blocking], NOT NB; timeout_ms >= 0 explicit, ANIRA_WAIT_CONTRACT, ANIRA_WAIT_FOREVER;
        semaphore wait when wait_ratio > 0, 1 ms poll otherwise (Core.cpp:1395-1464); a block not completed at the timeout is ANIRA_MISSED;
        ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_STATE at once without an inference thread inside its loop; Wasm: spins; a push never waits and has no twin ---- */
anira_status anira_handler_process_wait(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* in, uint32_t in_slot, const anira_tensor* out, uint32_t out_slot, size_t* delivered, double timeout_ms);
anira_status anira_handler_process_multi_wait(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* inputs, uint32_t num_inputs, const anira_tensor* outputs, uint32_t num_outputs, size_t* delivered, double timeout_ms);
anira_status anira_handler_pop_data_wait(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* out, uint32_t slot, size_t* delivered, double timeout_ms);
anira_status anira_handler_pop_data_multi_wait(anira_handler*, const anira_tensor* outputs, uint32_t num_outputs, size_t* delivered, double timeout_ms);

/* ================= abi/config.h — job options: the frame-invariant part of a job; the per-job deadline is a submit parameter;
                     the completion typedef and its setter land with abi/ticket.h (M3), the frame-invariant setters are M1 ======= */
typedef struct anira_job_options anira_job_options;
typedef void (ANIRA_CALL* anira_job_complete_fn)(anira_handler* h, anira_ticket t, anira_ticket_status s, void* user_data) NB;   /* [inference-thread] (IMMEDIATE) or the caller of poll/ticket_wait (POLLED); may call [cs] entries incl. ticket_release; never submit */
anira_status anira_job_options_create(anira_job_options** out, anira_error* err);              /* [main-thread]; build once, reuse across submits; never mutate concurrently with a submit that reads it */
anira_status anira_job_options_set_head_trim(anira_job_options*, uint32_t count, const int64_t* trims);   /* -1 = per-output latency (input-aligned) */
anira_status anira_job_options_set_tail_flush(anira_job_options*, anira_bool);                 /* ViewChunker reassembly semantics; default true */
anira_status anira_job_options_set_below_min(anira_job_options*, anira_pad_policy);            /* default ANIRA_PAD_REJECT */
anira_status anira_job_options_set_on_complete(anira_job_options*, anira_job_complete_fn, void* user_data);
anira_status anira_job_options_set_ext(anira_job_options*, const anira_ext_header*);            /* borrowed until submit returns; ext "crop_affine", "job_backend", "job_model" when their consumers arrive */
anira_status anira_job_options_set_ext_json(anira_job_options*, const char* kind, const char* utf8, size_t len);   /* the JSON twin every config handle carries, section 1b */
void         anira_job_options_destroy(anira_job_options*);

/* ================= abi/ticket.h — Async entries (M3) ====================================== */
typedef uint32_t anira_ticket;               /* slot (low 16) | generation (high 16); value type; in abi/enums.h since M2 */
#define ANIRA_TICKET_INVALID 0u                  /* with the other pinned sentinels in abi/enums.h, so anira_processor_ctx and anira_engine_ctx carry it at M2 */
anira_status anira_handler_allocate_input (anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, anira_domain, anira_tensor* out, anira_error* err);   /* [main-thread & prepared]; anira-owned pool tensor; anira never calls its release */
anira_status anira_handler_allocate_output(anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, anira_domain, anira_tensor* out, anira_error* err);
anira_status anira_handler_free_tensor(anira_handler*, anira_tensor*);                          /* [main-thread & prepared]; returns an allocate_* tensor to the pool; prepare/destroy free the rest */
anira_status anira_handler_bind_output(anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, anira_tensor*, anira_error* err);   /* [main-thread & prepared]; user-owned destination; acquire = writable-when; owning tokens move */
anira_status anira_handler_submit(anira_handler*, anira_tensor* inputs, uint32_t num_inputs, const anira_job_options* opts_or_null, double deadline_ms, void* job_user_data, anira_ticket* out) NB;
        /* [thread-safe]; owning acquire tokens move into the slot (caller's kind = NONE); deadline_ms absolute on anira_now_ms(), < 0 = the contract's; job_user_data stored by value, never dereferenced, read back with ticket_user_data; ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY when no slot is free; ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG when a tensor's dtype or axis-tag sequence disagrees with its spec, recorded in anira_handler_rt_error; *out is ANIRA_TICKET_INVALID on every failure; never blocks, never allocates */
uint32_t            anira_handler_poll(anira_handler*);                                          /* [main-thread]; drains Polled completions (on_complete, tensor release) on the calling thread; returns count */
anira_ticket_status anira_handler_ticket_status(const anira_handler*, anira_ticket) NB;          /* [thread-safe] [cs]; PENDING | MET | LATE | DROPPED | FAILED */
anira_ticket_status anira_handler_ticket_wait(anira_handler*, anira_ticket, double timeout_ms);  /* [any-thread, blocking]; any timeout_ms < 0 waits forever -- ANIRA_WAIT_FOREVER, and ANIRA_WAIT_CONTRACT, which has no block cadence to scale under Async; a stale or released ticket returns ANIRA_TICKET_FAILED; Wasm: spins */
anira_status anira_handler_ticket_input_released(const anira_handler*, anira_ticket, uint32_t i, anira_sync_token* out) NB;   /* [thread-safe] [cs]; non-owning view valid until ticket_release; dup with anira_sync_token_dup */
anira_status anira_handler_ticket_output_ready (const anira_handler*, anira_ticket, uint32_t i, anira_sync_token* out) NB;
anira_status anira_handler_ticket_error(const anira_handler*, anira_ticket, anira_error* out) NB;   /* [thread-safe] [cs]; FAILED reason, e.g. the kind name of an unconsumed job extension */
anira_status anira_handler_ticket_user_data(const anira_handler*, anira_ticket, void** out) NB;   /* [thread-safe] [cs]; the job_user_data of the submit that minted this ticket; ANIRA_ERROR_TICKET_STALE for a recycled slot */
void         anira_handler_ticket_release(anira_handler*, anira_ticket) NB;                      /* [thread-safe] [cs]; marks the slot for recycling (deferred until terminal; fds closed by the inference thread); legal from on_complete */
```

The same surface in `anira.hpp` (header-only, C++20, `namespace anira`, not ABI-stable; nothing in it is exported from libanira). `Hard`, `Async`, `Contract` and `ContractHandle` are section 3's, `Context` and `ContextConfig` are section 4's; what follows is the handler half and the types a processor or a custom engine subclasses. The Async half (`Ticket`, the allocate, submit and poll members, `JobOptions::on_complete`) lands with `abi/ticket.h` at M3 and is marked so:

```cpp
struct Error : std::runtime_error { anira_status status; };          // thrown by every control path on ANIRA_FAILED; the Hard entries return their status instead
template <class T> struct Result { T value{}; anira_error error{}; bool ok() const noexcept; };   // declared for the exception-free mode of a later pre-release: ANIRA_CXX_NO_EXCEPTIONS is an #error in this one
struct JobOptions { std::vector<int64_t> head_trim; bool tail_flush = true; anira_pad_policy below_min = ANIRA_PAD_REJECT; };   // frame-invariant, minted once by JobOptionsHandle;
                                                                     // on_complete (std::function<void(Ticket, anira_ticket_status)>) joins at M3; the deadline and the per-job void* are submit arguments
class Capabilities { std::vector<BackendId> backends() const; std::vector<Domain> domains() const; std::vector<std::string> ext_kinds() const;
                     std::vector<anira_edge_info> edges() const; anira_edge_info edge(Domain, const BackendId&) const; };
struct PlanQuery { struct Backend { EngineKind engine = ANIRA_ENGINE_NONE; Provider provider = ANIRA_PROVIDER_NONE; const char* engine_id = nullptr; const char* provider_id = nullptr; };
                   uint32_t variant = ANIRA_VARIANT_ANY; Backend backend{}; };   // anira_plan_query: NONE and ANY match every plan
class PlanReport { uint32_t num_plans() const noexcept; std::vector<anira_plan_info> plans() const;   // the budget is the row field anira_plan_info::budget_ms
                   std::vector<anira_plan_slot> slots(uint32_t plan, bool inputs) const; std::vector<anira_plan_ext> extensions(uint32_t plan) const;
                   std::optional<uint32_t> find(const PlanQuery&) const noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING; };   // plan 0 is the only plan of a single-candidate pipeline
class RingView { DType dtype() const noexcept; uint32_t num_channels() const noexcept; size_t available(uint32_t) const noexcept; size_t available_past(uint32_t) const noexcept;
                 template <class T> size_t pop_block(uint32_t, std::span<T>) noexcept;  template <class T> size_t peek_past_block(uint32_t, std::span<T>) const noexcept;
                 template <class T> size_t push_block(uint32_t, std::span<T>) noexcept;  template <class T> size_t push_fill(uint32_t, const T&, size_t) noexcept;
                 size_t discard(uint32_t, size_t) noexcept;                    // no dtype: discard moves the read position only
                 template <class T> size_t pop_windows(uint32_t, std::span<T>, size_t num_new, size_t num_old, size_t offset, uint32_t num_batches) noexcept; };
                 // over anira_ring_*, T -> anira_dtype at compile time: a T other than dtype() moves nothing and records ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG, never a conversion
class ProcessorContext { anira_phase phase() const noexcept; EngineRef engine() const noexcept; ProviderRef provider() const noexcept; uint32_t variant() const noexcept;
                         uint32_t num_inputs() const noexcept; uint32_t num_outputs() const noexcept; anira_ticket ticket() const noexcept; uint32_t entry() const noexcept;
                         anira_status input_role(uint32_t, Role&) const noexcept;  anira_status input_ring(uint32_t, RingView&) const noexcept;  anira_status input_tensor(uint32_t, Tensor&) const noexcept;
                         /* and output_role, output_ring, output_tensor */ };   // over the anira_processor_ctx POD and its accessors
class InitInfo { anira_log_level log_level() const noexcept; uint32_t num_threads() const noexcept; const anira_context* context() const noexcept; };   // over anira_init_info
class PrepareInfo { anira_handler* handler() const noexcept; PlanReport report() const noexcept; uint32_t num_entries() const noexcept; bool exclusive() const noexcept;
                    std::span<const Tensor> inputs() const noexcept; std::span<const char* const> input_names() const noexcept; /* and the outputs */ };   // over anira_prepare_info
class Processor { virtual uint32_t phases() const noexcept = 0;  virtual uint32_t flags() const noexcept;   // k_pre_process | ...; ANIRA_PROCESSOR_FLAG_REALTIME_*
                  virtual std::span<const char* const> consumed_kinds() const noexcept;  virtual void init(const InitInfo&);
                  virtual std::unique_ptr<Prepared> prepare(const PrepareInfo&) = 0;  virtual void release() noexcept;
    class Prepared { virtual anira_status pre_process(ProcessorContext&) noexcept { return anira_processor_default_pre_process(...); }
                     // and post_process (anira_processor_default_post_process), before_inference, after_inference (ANIRA_OK), reset(ProcessorContext&) (nothing)
    };                                                                 // the defaults forward to anira_processor_default_*: "call super" is the base class; one Prepared per handler
};                                                                     // no name: a pipeline holds one processor
class EngineLoadInfo { /* row(), model(), model_count(), model_engine(i), model_path(i), model_bytes(i), inputs(), outputs(), input_names(), output_names(),
                          instances(), provider(), option_keys(), option_values() -- over anira_engine_load_info */ };
class EngineContext { std::span<const Tensor> inputs() const noexcept; std::span<Tensor> outputs() const noexcept; };   // over the anira_engine_ctx POD
class Engine { explicit Engine(std::string id); const std::string& id() const noexcept;   // the reverse-URI id; the object is the identity loaded models are shared by
               virtual uint32_t flags() const noexcept;  virtual std::span<const char* const> consumed_kinds() const noexcept;  virtual std::span<const char* const> providers() const noexcept;
               virtual std::uint64_t query(const InitInfo&) const;  virtual void init(const InitInfo&);  virtual std::unique_ptr<Loaded> load(const EngineLoadInfo&) = 0;  virtual void release() noexcept;
    class Loaded { virtual std::unique_ptr<Prepared> prepare(const PrepareInfo&) = 0; };   // one per loaded model, shared by the handlers on it
    class Prepared { virtual anira_status process(EngineContext&) noexcept = 0;  virtual void reset(EngineContext&) noexcept; };   // one per handler and loaded model
};
class Pipeline { Pipeline();                                           // anira_pipeline_create; move-only
                 Pipeline& add_inference(const ModelConfig&, std::initializer_list<BackendId> candidates = {});
                 Pipeline& add_inference(std::initializer_list<std::reference_wrapper<const ModelConfig>> variants, std::initializer_list<BackendId> candidates);
                 Pipeline& add_processor(const std::shared_ptr<Processor>&);   // at most one: the C entry refuses a second
                 Pipeline& add_engine(const std::shared_ptr<Engine>&);         // one C engine per Engine object for as long as anything holds it
                 PipelineCapabilities capabilities(const Context&) const noexcept; };   // anira_pipeline_capabilities_backends / _edge
class Ticket { anira_ticket_status status() const noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING; anira_ticket_status wait(); anira_ticket_status wait_for(std::chrono::nanoseconds);   // M3
               SyncToken input_released(uint32_t) const; SyncToken output_ready(uint32_t) const; anira_error error() const;
               void* user_data() const noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;   // anira_handler_ticket_user_data: the per-job void* this ticket was submitted with
               ~Ticket(); /* releases the slot */ };
class InferenceHandler {                                               // move-only; the destructor is anira_handler_destroy
    InferenceHandler(Context&, const Pipeline&);                       // throws Error
    PlanReport prepare(const ContractHandle&);  PlanReport prepare(const Hard&);  PlanReport prepare(const Async&);  PlanReport prepare(const Contract&);   // throws Error
    PlanReport plan_report() const noexcept;  uint32_t num_entries() const noexcept;
    // Hard entries: noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING one-line forwarders onto the tensor entries; a status returned, never thrown; never wait
    anira_status process(const Tensor& in, uint32_t in_slot, const Tensor& out, uint32_t out_slot, size_t* delivered = nullptr) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;
    anira_status process(std::span<const Tensor> inputs, std::span<const Tensor> outputs, size_t* delivered = nullptr) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;   // the _multi entry
    anira_status push_data(const Tensor& in, uint32_t slot) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;  anira_status push_data(std::span<const Tensor>) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;
    anira_status pop_data(const Tensor& out, uint32_t slot, size_t* delivered = nullptr) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;  anira_status pop_data(std::span<const Tensor>, size_t* = nullptr) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;
    // _wait twins: noexcept, blocking; timeout_ms >= 0 or the ANIRA_WAIT_* sentinels
    anira_status process_wait(const Tensor& in, uint32_t in_slot, const Tensor& out, uint32_t out_slot, double timeout_ms, size_t* delivered = nullptr) noexcept;   // and the span form
    anira_status pop_data_wait(const Tensor& out, uint32_t slot, double timeout_ms, size_t* delivered = nullptr) noexcept;   // and the span form
    anira_status set_static_input(uint32_t slot, const Tensor&) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;  anira_status get_static_output(uint32_t slot, const Tensor& out) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;
    uint32_t get_latency(uint32_t slot = 0) const noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;  std::vector<uint32_t> get_latencies() const;
    anira_status get_available_samples(uint32_t slot, uint32_t channel, size_t* out) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;   // non-const: runs post_process
    void reset() noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;  anira_status rt_error() const noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;
    anira_status set_plan(uint32_t plan) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;  uint32_t get_plan() const noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;   // the whole selection surface: one relaxed store of the index PlanReport::find gives
    // Async entries (M3)
    Tensor allocate_input(uint32_t slot, Domain);  Tensor allocate_output(uint32_t slot, Domain);  void free_tensor(Tensor&);  void bind_output(uint32_t slot, Tensor&);
    Ticket submit(std::span<Tensor> inputs, const JobOptions& = {}, std::optional<std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point> deadline = {}, void* job_user_data = nullptr) noexcept(false);   // tokens move; ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY -> Error; the pointer comes back as Ticket::user_data()
    uint32_t poll();
    anira_handler* native() noexcept; };
class InferenceThread { explicit InferenceThread(Context&); void start(); void stop() noexcept; bool execute() noexcept; void run_loop() noexcept;
                        bool should_exit() const noexcept; bool is_running() const noexcept; bool has_exited() const noexcept; };
std::vector<BackendId> enabled_engines();  uint32_t num_inference_threads() noexcept;  size_t drain_log() noexcept;   // inline, over the C entries
anira_status shutdown() noexcept;  bool release_core_if_idle() noexcept;  bool has_core() noexcept;  double now_ms() noexcept;  uint64_t now_ns() noexcept;
inline void log_rt(anira_log_level, const char* group, const char* static_message, int32_t = 0, int32_t = 0) noexcept ANIRA_NONBLOCKING;
```

`prepare` takes one object, the contract, and the edge-cost policy travels inside it: `anira_contract_set_edge_cost(c, ANIRA_EDGE_COST_PERMISSIVE | STRICT)` is plan validation, not scheduling, and it rides on the contract handle because the contract file already carries `edge_cost` (section 8) and a second `prepare` parameter would exist only to mirror a separate policy object; `anira.hpp` keeps it as the `edge_cost` member of `Hard` and `Async`, so `handler.prepare(Hard{.host_block_min = 512, .host_block_max = 512, .host_rate = 48000, .budget = 5ms, .warmup = ANIRA_WARMUP_FIXED, .warmup_iterations = 2})` compiles, validates every candidate's plan and returns the `PlanReport` (section 7). In this pre-release `prepare` refuses an Async contract, `ANIRA_BUDGET_MEASURED` and `ANIRA_WARMUP_UNTIL_STABLE` with `ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED` (the estimator is M2b PR 4), so a Hard contract spells an explicit budget and a FIXED or NONE warm-up. `prepare` is the blocking quiescence point, never from the driver thread; a `prepare` while processing is a contract error. The report is handler-owned, valid until the next `prepare` or `destroy`, walked by stride-explicit enumerators (`anira_plan_report_plans`, `anira_plan_report_slots`, `anira_plan_report_exts`) and searched by `anira_plan_report_find`; `anira.hpp` copies the rows into vectors and spells the search `PlanReport::find`.

The Hard entries run the core's Hard path over host tensors (`src/capi/handler.cpp:2660-2784` into `InferenceManager::process_nowait`, `push_data` and `pop_data`, `src/scheduler/InferenceManager.cpp:328-471`), which reads the 3.x model config, the contract snapshot and `capi::Derived` directly since PR 10b (`v3_to_v2` is gone); they carry a truthful `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`, and none of them waits. v2's in-callback wait -- `blocking_ratio > 0` inside `process()` (`v2.3.0:src/scheduler/InferenceManager.cpp:59-73`), and every Hard entry under `set_non_realtime` (`v2.3.0:src/scheduler/Context.cpp:947-962`) -- lives in the `_wait` twins, which are not `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`: `timeout_ms >= 0` is explicit, `ANIRA_WAIT_CONTRACT` (-2.0) waits `wait_ratio × block duration` and is v2's `blocking_ratio` behaviour, `ANIRA_WAIT_FOREVER` (-1.0) is v2's `set_non_realtime` behaviour; with `wait_ratio > 0` they wait on the completion semaphore, with `wait_ratio == 0` they poll the done atomic with 1 ms sleeps (`Core::new_data_request`, `src/scheduler/Core.cpp:1395-1464`), a block not completed at the timeout is `ANIRA_MISSED`, and without an inference thread inside its loop they run the nonblocking stem and return `ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_STATE` at once. They are `[any-thread, blocking]`: legal from the driver thread only if the host accepts a wait there. `anira_handler_set_non_realtime` is not a C entry: `compat/v2.hpp` implements `v2::InferenceHandler::set_non_realtime(bool)` as a wrapper-side flag that routes the v2 overloads to the `_wait` twins with `ANIRA_WAIT_FOREVER`, and to `ANIRA_WAIT_CONTRACT` when the legacy contract carries `blocking_ratio > 0` -- v2-identical from the compat layer, including the latency figure (`get_latency` reports the v2 arithmetic with the wait credit; a host that calls the NB `process` on a `wait_ratio > 0` handler gets the same latency figure and more `on_miss` events). On Wasm every `_wait` twin spins; calling one inside the worklet is the host's decision, as v2's `blocking_ratio` was.

The forced changes against the 2.x signatures: one host tensor per slot replaces the `float* const*` channel arrays and their sample counts (a float host builds one planar tensor over its pointer array once, with `anira_tensor_init_host_planar`, and stores `shape[1]` before each call); the `_multi` arrays have the lengths of the model's two lists and the delivered counts come back through `size_t* delivered`, a pure out-parameter, instead of a handler-owned `size_t*` (`v2.3.0:include/anira/InferenceHandler.h:232-235`, which leaked libc++ layout into the web wrapper); indices are `uint32_t` slots (`slot`, `in_slot`, `out_slot`), one slot space for every entry, the `_multi` arrays and `anira_miss_fn`; `anira_handler_get_available_samples` returns a status, takes a non-const handler and is `[driver-thread]` because it collects the completed inferences (`InferenceManager::get_available_samples`, `src/scheduler/InferenceManager.cpp:738-747`) and therefore runs the processor's `post_process`. The latency vector is index-aligned with the output list and reports 0 for a Static or State output, as 2.x did since the one-sided streaming fix (`v2.3.0:include/anira/InferenceHandler.h:347-367`): a C array indexed by slot is the only representation that cannot misalign. Static tensors are materialised by anira, not by a processor: the value set through `anira_handler_set_static_input` is copied into the model's input tensor before the processor's `pre_process` runs, and a Static output is captured from the model's output tensor after `post_process`, independent of the processor -- the store is the handler's, zeroed at `anira_handler_create` and left alone by `prepare` and `reset`, and `anira_processor_ctx` is a frozen 64-byte POD without a handler pointer, so no processor can reach it otherwise (v2 did this inside the default `PrePostProcessor`, `v2.3.0:src/PrePostProcessor.cpp:13-31, 60-71, 85-93`). There are no scalar Static entries: the whole tensor in the spec's shape and dtype is the one spelling, so a dtype other than the spec's is refused with `ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG`, never converted, and an `int64` class index is readable like any other value. It is also the non-tearing read: `anira_handler_get_static_output` copies the value the latest collected inference produced under the slot's latch, so a classifier vector cannot mix two inferences. Both entries are legal from `anira_handler_create` on, prepared or not, a processor's `prepare` may call them, and every `_multi` form takes a Static slot's whole tensor, or the empty tensor, as its element.

RT entries carry no `anira_error` (section 6a); `anira_handler_rt_error(h)` is where a worklet reads why a Hard entry failed. Calling a Hard entry on an Async handler returns `ANIRA_ERROR_WRONG_CONTRACT` (a delivered count of 0) and records it in `rt_error`; in this pre-release an Async contract is already refused at `prepare`. `rt_error` is last-wins and cleared by `prepare` and `reset`; besides the entries' contract violations it records `ANIRA_ERROR_ENGINE` after a failed inference and `ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY` after a hop the scheduler dropped because no inference slot was free (the overload contract of section 3: the hop is filled under `on_miss` at its stream position), while an entry's own `CAPACITY` return is back-pressure and never lands there. Each kind emits one latched record through the RT queue: per handler and per status kind the first occurrence after `prepare`/`reset` is logged at Error, a contract violation flagged `ANIRA_LOG_RECORD_CONTRACT_VIOLATION`, later ones increment a suppressed counter with one relaxed RMW, the drain thread reports changed counters at most every 10 s, and the re-arm logs the final count; the type-level Hard/Async coupling of section 3 is documented by `anira.hpp`'s types and enforced here at runtime. Hard's payload is host memory the caller owns -- one host `anira_tensor` per slot through `process`/`push_data`/`pop_data` (HOST or HOST_PINNED, rank 2 `[channels, samples]`, planar, strided or packed, const, never written), or the whole Static tensor through `set_static_input`/`get_static_output` -- and every device-handle entry (`allocate_input`, `allocate_output`, `bind_output`, `submit`) is an Async entry. That, not any dtype rule, is why real-time video is Async with a deadline and `ANIRA_LATE_DROP`: a frame that stays on the GPU has no Hard entry to arrive through, and one that reaches host memory has no shape to arrive in, because the Hard entries and the rings beneath them carry `(channel, time)` scalars and a two-dimensional frame has nowhere to live in a ring -- the accumulate-to-full column of the chunker table (section 7) is the `submit` path, not this one.

Runtime selection is one store, the same for both contracts: `anira_handler_set_plan(h, plan)` takes the dense plan index `anira_plan_report_plans` handed out or `anira_plan_report_find` found, is a relaxed atomic store (`SessionElement::select_plan`, `src/scheduler/SessionElement.cpp:71-75`), `[thread-safe] ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`, atomic selection among the precompiled plan set (section 7), effective at a safe boundary -- the next submitted chunk under Hard, the next job under Async -- and never triggering planning; an index out of range is a no-op recorded as `ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG`. The boundary is a stamp, not a convention: the session's selection is one atomic holding the dense plan index (`SessionElement::m_current_plan`), the chunk takes it with one load when it is submitted (`Core::pre_process` writes the index on the chunk's struct, `src/scheduler/Core.cpp:1496-1504`), and its `pre_process`, `before_inference`, engine call, `after_inference` and `post_process` all resolve the stamp through the session's plan table and never read the atomic again, so a chunk that is queued or in flight when the store lands finishes on the plan it started on and exactly one engine runs for it. The index is what is stored and stamped, never the engine: two variants on one engine, or one engine on two providers, are two plans that an engine value cannot tell apart. `anira_handler_get_plan` loads the same atomic back, so there is no second value for concurrent callers to tear. The plan table is the session's (`SessionElement::m_plans`: dense index -> the loaded model and the session's prepared handle over it), built when `prepare` creates the session and never changed afterwards, so the driving thread and the inference threads read it without synchronisation; the engine `switch` of the 2.x runtime is gone. Two independent stores on two axes could land on a pair that has no plan, on the driver thread, with no error channel; one store cannot. `set_plan` keeps the model's state: every plan of the one variant runs the same declared State pairs, so the next chunk reads the state the last one produced, whichever plan produced it; clearing stream state is a variant switch (M2b PR V). `anira.hpp` has no `set_model`/`set_backend` through M2: a host looks the plan of a backend choice up with `PlanReport::find` and stores it with `set_plan`, and the two conveniences arrive with the variant axis (M2b PR V); `compat/v2.hpp` implements v2's `set_inference_backend` as the first plan on that backend, a backend without a plan keeping the selection and logging once.

Async entries. `anira_ticket` is a `uint32_t` value -- slot in the low 16 bits, generation in the high 16, `ANIRA_TICKET_INVALID = 0` -- never a heap object: `submit` never allocates and never blocks, `ticket_release` marks the slot for recycling, `ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY` arrives when no slot is free and `ANIRA_ERROR_TICKET_STALE` when a released slot is addressed again; `anira.hpp`'s `Ticket` is the RAII owner whose destructor releases. Ownership, token moves and the `[callback-safe]` list are section 6a's; `submit` and `bind_output` take `anira_tensor*` (non-const) because that is where the owning tokens move. The per-job deadline is a scalar parameter of `submit` (`double deadline_ms`, absolute on the `anira_now_ms()` clock of `abi/context.h`, `< 0` = the contract's relative deadline) because it is a presentation timestamp set every frame from the render thread, and a render loop with a 33 ms deadline must not be the thread that touches an allocating setter; `anira_job_options` therefore holds only the frame-invariant part, is built once and reused across submits, and `anira.hpp`'s `JobOptionsHandle` mints its handle once from a `JobOptions` for the same reason. The per-job `void* job_user_data` is a scalar parameter of `submit` for exactly that reason: it is the per-frame channel for a presentation timestamp, a crop rectangle or frame identity, which `anira_job_options` cannot carry because it is frame-invariant and its setters allocate on the main thread. anira stores it by value in the slot and never dereferences it; `anira_handler_ticket_user_data(h, t, &p)` `[thread-safe]` `[callback-safe]` `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` reads it back, so a completion running on the inference thread learns which frame it just finished. `anira_job_complete_fn` is unchanged -- its `user_data` stays the options-level one, and the two never merge. A submitted tensor whose `anira_dtype` or `anira_axis_tag` sequence disagrees with its `anira_tensor_spec` is refused at `submit` with `ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG`, recorded in `anira_handler_rt_error` and minting no ticket; conversion, if it ever arrives, is a processor. Delivery splits where the completion runs, not what it may do: `on_complete` fires on the inference thread under `ANIRA_DELIVERY_IMMEDIATE` and on the caller of `poll`/`ticket_wait` under `ANIRA_DELIVERY_POLLED`, and `ticket_release` from inside it is legal while a re-entrant `submit` is not. `request_output` (an anira-owned output pool with recycling on release) stays deferred past v1; when it arrives it is one appended function under ABI major 3.

Any handle is accepted at `submit` and `bind_output`; the plan decides what it costs. `allocate_input`/`allocate_output` exist because the fast rows of section 7 are decided at allocation time and anira can retrofit nothing: they hand back a handle in the user's own API -- a `VkBuffer` the user's shader writes as an SSBO, a GL buffer object, or for a WebGPU candidate a dma-buf-backed renderbuffer the user renders into -- allocated so that every enabled candidate gets its best row. The `PlanReport` states per slot the edge taken, its class, the class an `allocate_*` handle would have gotten, and why they differ (`anira_plan_slot.edge_class`, `.allocate_class`, `.recipe`, `.reason`). A foreign handle that works but slower is data there, never a log line; one that cannot work at all (a same-process image never exported, a `WGPUBuffer` of another device) fails `prepare()` with the recipe in `anira_error.message`. `ANIRA_EDGE_COST_STRICT` makes the first case fail too -- the harness rule that an unavailable zero-copy row is a bug, not a fallback, promoted to a library contract; development runs strict, production ships permissive.

Push/pop vs submit/poll: the same exchange addressed by stream position (quantitative, continuous; the miss policy, or the host's `anira_miss_fn`, fills a missed block) versus ticket identity (transactional, out-of-order across lanes, never fabricates). Below the API both feed the same pump.

---

## 6a. The C ABI

The binary contract of anira v3 is a set of C11 headers, `include/anira/abi/*.h`, and the flat `anira_*` functions they declare. Everything else that ships -- `anira.hpp`, `compat/v2.hpp`, the TypeScript package -- is a convenience over that set and promises nothing on its own. This section is the contract's rulebook: what is frozen, by which number, in which file, and which gate catches a violation. Section 6b states the boundary to tanh-lib and the browser; section 11 the gates' schedule.

### Principles

Each rule is enforceable; the gate that enforces it (section 11) is named in brackets.

1. The header set `include/anira/abi/*.h` is the library's only binary contract, byte-identical for native shared, native static (plugins, iOS) and Emscripten; `anira.hpp` and the TS package are conveniences over it. [gate 4: the header set compiles as C11 on every CI leg; gate 5: a pure-C consumer links both library shapes]
2. Every entry point is a directly exported `anira_*` C function with a frozen signature; no function table, no `uintptr_t` handle, no exported C++ symbol from v3.0.0-alpha.2 on, on ELF, Mach-O, PE and Wasm alike. [gate 1: symbol baseline on ELF/Mach-O/PE; the `--emit-tsd` export diff on Wasm]
3. The Hard path is frozen: the `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` entries `anira_handler_process/process_multi/push_data*/pop_data*/reset` take one `const anira_tensor*` per slot, return an `anira_status` (`ANIRA_OK`, `ANIRA_MISSED` or a failure) and never wait; they run the core's Hard path (`src/capi/handler.cpp:2660-2784`, `src/scheduler/InferenceManager.cpp:328-471`) on the 3.x model config, the contract snapshot and `capi::Derived`; v2's in-callback wait (`blocking_ratio`, `set_non_realtime`) lives in the `_wait` twins, which are not `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`. [the 2.x suites run through `anira.hpp`/`compat/v2.hpp`; gate 6 runs the whole suite under RTSan]
4. Nothing on an `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` path allocates, locks, waits, formats into heap memory, touches thread-local storage, returns a heap object, or invokes a callback that may; `rt_vsnprintf` into a fixed record is the one allowed RT formatting. A `thread_local` lives on a control path only; `src/` has two, the firewall's failure-path depth guard (`src/capi/error.cpp:31`) and the log dispatch's running-sink guard (`src/utils/LogSinks.cpp:56`). On Wasm a `thread_local` is per thread as it is natively: every inference Worker's region holds its own stack and thread-local block (`src/platform/emscripten/em_worker.cpp:174-183`), and the Audio Worklet thread gets its own the same way, on the region anira hands `emscripten_start_wasm_audio_worklet_thread_async` (`src/platform/emscripten/em_audio.cpp:764-777`). [gate 6: the whole suite under RTSan; `-Werror=function-effects` on the consumer-shaped targets]
5. RT data is fixed-layout POD with one `sizeof`/offset table on every target (`ANIRA_PTR` pins every pointer slot to 8 bytes), and that table is committed data (`abi/layout-<major>.txt`, `layout-0.txt` through the pre-releases) that `tools/abi/gen.py` writes from the registry and gate 3 verifies; from v3.0.0 only an ABI-major commit may change it; everything with a lifetime is an opaque handle mutated by scalar setters. [gate 3: layout executable natively and under node, diffed against the committed table]
6. No enum value, struct layout or function set depends on a build option: `USE_*`, `ENABLE_LOGGING` and `ANIRA_VERSION` are PUBLIC on the object library `anira_objects` and reach `anira` only as `$<BUILD_INTERFACE:>` for the bundled consumers (`CMakeLists.txt:515-535`), so the installed `anira::anira` carries none of them; presence is a runtime query (`anira_enabled_engines`). [gate 4 compiles every header with no anira define at all]
7. Errors are `anira_status` integers; `status < 0` is the only stable failure test; messages land in a caller-owned `anira_error`; exceptions stop in `src/capi/`. No status objects, no thread-local last-error, no `errno`; `== ANIRA_OK` is not a stable test because a minor may add positive statuses. [gate 5; every control-path entry is `ANIRA_NOEXCEPT` and a function-try-block whose handler is `anira::capi::translate_exception(err, __func__)` (`src/capi/capi_internal.h`); `anira.hpp` and the generated TS bindings test the sign (`ANIRA_FAILED`)]
8. Append-only inside an ABI major: new functions, enum values, extension revisions, `anira_em_*` companions and descriptor tail slots appended after the last v3.0.0 slot bump `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR`; a changed layout, signature, value, thread contract or RT class is an ABI major bump. [gate 2, with the freeze: abidiff bit 8 always fails; bit 4 fails unless the minor moved since the last tag; the Tier-1 abidiff fails on bit 4 regardless. Since M1 the registry diff, `tools/abi/gen.py --diff-against <last tag>`, classifies every change to `abi/anira.yml` at release time: an appended function, value or tail field is a minor or pre-release change; a removed or renamed promised entity, a changed value, signature, thread tag or Tier-1 layout, a demotion from promised and a removed `callback_safe` are breaking; a `status: draft` function is outside the promise, so whatever happens to it is admitted and its promotion is listed -- advisory while the major is 0, gating from v3.0.0]
9. Every declaration carries one thread tag from the fixed vocabulary below (the dual `[driver-thread | inference-thread]` of the processor callbacks and the processor-context entries is one tag of it), `[callback-safe]` as an additive property where it applies, and `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` where the body is RT. A callback typedef carries `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` only where its slot is RT by contract (`anira_miss_fn`); a processor's or an engine's real-time promise is its descriptor's flags (`ANIRA_PROCESSOR_FLAG_REALTIME_*`, `ANIRA_ENGINE_FLAG_REALTIME_SAFE`). [the generator `tools/abi/gen.py` refuses an entry whose tag is not in the vocabulary, a `[driver-thread]` or dual-tagged entry without `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` and a `[main-thread]`-family or `[any-thread, blocking]` one with it; the headers are generated from `abi/anira.yml` and never hand-edited]
10. tanh-lib, concurrentqueue and nlohmann_json are PRIVATE and invisible on every platform: tanh_core's objects are absorbed into libanira, no public header includes them, logging crosses as a C callback, tanh-lib gets no C ABI. [`anira_exports` forbids `thl`, `moodycamel`, `nlohmann`; strict `anira_header_isolation`; `consumer_tanh_first` proves the two copies separate for a shared libanira on ELF and Mach-O and for the prelinked static ELF one, while a plain static archive (Mach-O, PE) shares one copy with a host's tanh-lib (section 6b)]
11. JS never hand-computes a struct offset on the control path and never writes a struct field by hand: every descriptor JS must produce has an `anira_em_*_init` or a scalar-setter twin; RT structs are read through generated `layout.ts`. [`gen.py --check` (the `anira_abi_generate` CTest and `build_web.yml`) diffs the generated `web/src/abi/{enums,layout,bindings}.ts` and `exports_wasm.txt`; `build_web.yml` diffs the `.d.ts` exports against the symbol lists]
12. No struct-by-value parameter or return anywhere; no 64-bit integer in an `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` declaration (allowlist: `anira_now_ns`, the GPU tensor factories `anira_tensor_init_vulkan`, `_init_opaque_fd`, `_init_wgpu_buffer`, `_init_dmabuf` and the draft `_init_iosurface`, whose parameters are vendor handles, byte offsets and sizes at their wire width -- nothing that produces a `VkBuffer` runs in JS; `WIDE_INT_ALLOWLIST` in `tools/abi/gen.py:85-92` carries the six names); timeouts and deadlines are `double` milliseconds; indices and sentinels are `uint32_t`; sample and element counts are `size_t`. [`gen.py` validation]
13. Stability is declared once, at v3.0.0 (`ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR 3`); before it `ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR` is 0 (CLAP's "0.X.Y is development", `clap/include/clap/version.h:11-13`), gates 1 and 3-6 run, the registry diff is advisory and the abidiff arrives with the freeze. [`anira_check_abi` demands an exact match while major is 0]
14. Unmeasured surfaces live in `include/anira/abi/draft/` with their own baselines (`abi/symbols-draft.txt`, and `abi/abidiff-draft.supp` with gate 2), never included by `anira.h`; promotion moves a name between baselines and never renames it. [gate 1 treats both lists alike: a missing promised or draft name fails, and so does an export in neither]
15. Every CMake change uses the existing tanh-tooling modules; what they lack is an anira-local script under `anira/cmake/` (never `cmake/tanh/`, pin-owned) until a tanh-tooling tag after v3.0.0 that anira and tanh-lib re-pin together. [the `tooling-config` job of `lint.yml`, tanh-tooling's `config-check.yml` at v0.3.0: drift from the pin or a foreign file in `cmake/tanh/`]

### Header set

Files installed under `include/anira/`: exactly the list of `cmake/public-headers.cmake`, whose configure check fails on any header under `include/anira/` it does not list, plus the generated `abi/build_info.h`:

| file | content | frozen at |
|---|---|---|
| `anira.h` | C11 umbrella, generated from `abi/anira.yml`: includes every promised `abi/*.h` below in registry order, never `abi/draft/` or `anira_em.h`; through alpha.1 `include/anira/anira.h` was the 2.x C++ umbrella (`v2.3.0:include/anira/anira.h:4-22`) and the M1 headers were included per file | v3.0.0 |
| `anira_all.h` | `anira.h` plus `abi/draft/*.h` plus `anira_em.h` | -- |
| `abi/export.h` | `ANIRA_API`, `ANIRA_CALL`, `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`, `ANIRA_NOEXCEPT`, `ANIRA_DEPRECATED`, `ANIRA_INIT`, `ANIRA_PTR` | M1 |
| `abi/version.h`, `abi/build_info.h` | ABI constants and negotiation (`version.h` generated from the registry; `build_info.h` configured by `cmake/build-info.cmake` from `cmake/build_info.h.in`, which derives the ABI pair and the semver triple from the git tag -- the one source -- and installed beside the committed headers) | M1 / generated |
| `abi/status.h`, `abi/enums.h`, `abi/config.h`, `abi/log.h` | status codes, `anira_error` and the opaque handle types; every enum and the pinned sentinels; config handles and descriptors; log sink | M1 |
| `abi/tensor.h`, `abi/context.h`, `abi/core.h`, `abi/thread.h`, `abi/lifecycle.h`, `abi/processor.h`, `abi/engine.h`, `abi/handler.h` | RT PODs and the tensor factories; context, capabilities, the probe; the core's clock and shutdown family; user-driven inference threads; the init and prepare records a processor and an engine share; rings, the processor context and descriptor; the engine load record, context and descriptor; pipeline, custom engines, handler, plan report, Hard entries and their `_wait` twins | M2 |
| `abi/ticket.h` | Async entries | M3 |
| `abi/draft/*.h` | `draft/tensor_platform.h`: the unmeasured platform arms (`anira_tensor_init_metal/iosurface/ahardwarebuffer/d3d12`), later `frame.h`; included by `anira_all.h` only | never (until promoted) |
| `anira_em.h` | Emscripten companion, generated from `abi/anira.yml` (`platform: emscripten`), body under `#ifdef __EMSCRIPTEN__`; its names go to `abi/symbols-emscripten.txt` and `web/src/abi/exports_wasm.txt`, never to `abi/symbols-0.txt` (section 6b) | with `anira.h` |
| `anira.hpp` | header-only C++20 face, declared not ABI-stable | -- |
| `compat/v2.hpp` | `anira::v2::*` constructor shims and `anira::v2::PrePostProcessor` views (section 10); removed one minor after 3.0 | -- |

Per-file headers exist so the abidiff baseline and "which file froze when" are readable per milestone; naming the umbrella only at alpha.2 kept the 2.x suite compiling through alpha.1 without a rename. Naming: functions `anira_<object>_<verb>`, types `anira_<noun>` (`typedef struct anira_context anira_context;` -- handles are `anira_context*`, never the `uintptr_t` the 2.x web wrapper passed, `v2.3.0:src/emscripten-wrappers/InferenceHandler.cpp:10-42`), enum constants `ANIRA_<ENUM>_<VALUE>` with `ANIRA_<ENUM>_FORCE32 = 0x7fffffff`, macros `ANIRA_*`, one `ANIRA_<DESC>_INIT` default initialiser per descriptor. The generated headers wrap their declarations in `NOLINTBEGIN(readability-identifier-naming, modernize-use-using, bugprone-macro-parentheses)` and carry a nested `include/anira/abi/.clang-format` (`DisableFormat: true`: the generator is the formatter), which keeps `clang_tidy.yml` and the format check green without touching the pin-owned root configs; the workflow's positive `SOURCES` list names `src/capi/` and `test/abi/`. The headers carry no `_Static_assert` (those live in the generated `test/abi/generated/test_layout.c`), no bitfield and no function-like macro inside a declaration beyond `ANIRA_API`/`ANIRA_CALL`/`ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`/`ANIRA_NOEXCEPT`/`ANIRA_PTR`, so a `ctypes`/`cffi` binding needs the preprocessor and the committed `abi/layout-<major>.txt`, never a code generator.

### Export macros and what a consumer links

```c
/* abi/export.h (generated; self-contained: stdint.h only) */
#if defined(__EMSCRIPTEN__)
#  define ANIRA_API __attribute__((visibility("default")))   /* + the generated -sEXPORTED_FUNCTIONS (web/src/abi/exports_wasm.txt) */
#elif defined(ANIRA_STATIC)                                   /* PUBLIC on a static anira, cmake/tanh/symbol-policy.cmake:112-117 */
#  define ANIRA_API                                           /* hidden inside the embedding plugin */
#elif defined(_WIN32)
#  if defined(ANIRA_BUILDING)                                 /* PRIVATE, symbol-policy.cmake:111 */
#    define ANIRA_API __declspec(dllexport)                   /* dllexport IS the allowlist on PE, symbol-policy.cmake:180-182 */
#  else
#    define ANIRA_API __declspec(dllimport)
#  endif
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
#  define ANIRA_API __attribute__((visibility("default")))    /* + the version script of tanh_set_export_allowlist(anira SYMBOL "anira_*"), CMakeLists.txt:420 */
#else
#  define ANIRA_API
#endif
#if defined(_WIN32) && !defined(_WIN64)
#  define ANIRA_CALL __cdecl                                  /* CLAP_ABI, clap/include/clap/private/macros.h:20-26; fixed forever */
#else
#  define ANIRA_CALL
#endif
#if defined(__clang__) && defined(__has_attribute)
#  if __has_attribute(nonblocking)
#    define ANIRA_NONBLOCKING __attribute__((nonblocking))    /* upstream clang 20 or later and emcc 4.0.23, in C11 and C++20 */
#  endif
#endif
#ifndef ANIRA_NONBLOCKING
#  define ANIRA_NONBLOCKING
#endif
#if defined(__cplusplus)
#  define ANIRA_NOEXCEPT noexcept
#  define ANIRA_INIT(type, ...) (type{__VA_ARGS__})
#else
#  define ANIRA_NOEXCEPT
#  define ANIRA_INIT(type, ...) ((type){__VA_ARGS__})
#endif
#define ANIRA_PTR(T, name) union { T* name; uint64_t name##_bits; }   /* 8-byte pointer slot on ILP32 and LP64; zero the struct before use */
#if defined(_MSC_VER) && !defined(__clang__)
#  define ANIRA_DEPRECATED(msg) __declspec(deprecated(msg))
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
#  define ANIRA_DEPRECATED(msg) __attribute__((deprecated(msg)))
#else
#  define ANIRA_DEPRECATED(msg)
#endif
```

Every declaration is `ANIRA_API <ret> ANIRA_CALL name(...) ANIRA_NOEXCEPT`, with `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` after it where the body is RT. `ANIRA_STATIC`/`ANIRA_BUILDING` come from `tanh_apply_symbol_policy(... EXPORT_PREFIX ANIRA)` on `anira_objects` and on `anira` (`CMakeLists.txt:324`, `:416`); `abi/export.h` is self-contained and includes nothing of tanh-lib, where the 2.x `system/Exports.h` included `<tanh/core/ExportMacros.h>` (`v2.3.0:include/anira/system/Exports.h:30`). `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` is gated on `__clang__` + `__has_attribute`, never on `ANIRA_WITH_RTSAN`, and replaced the 2.x internal `ANIRA_REALTIME` (`v2.3.0:include/anira/utils/RealtimeSanitizer.h:4-14`), so the public and the internal attribute are one definition; the private `src/utils/RealtimeSanitizer.h` keeps only `ANIRA_BLOCKING`. No C++ class or function carries `ANIRA_API`: `system/Exports.h`, `AniraExports.h` and `AniraWinExports.h` are deleted, the core's C++ headers are private under `src/` and never installed, and the only exports are the `anira_*` C entries (gate 1 strict on ELF, Mach-O and PE), so no decorated internal class can stay in `anira-0.dll`'s export table after the version script has stripped it from ELF and Mach-O.

A static-embedded consumer links `anira::anira`, a self-contained archive with tanh_core's objects inside it (section 6b). On ELF with a single-configuration generator and `readelf`/`objcopy` at hand, the static libanira is one prelinked relocatable object (`cmake/prelink-archive.cmake`, `CMakeLists.txt:326-400`) in which every hidden symbol but the `anira_*` entries is local, anira's C++ internals and tanh-lib's alike: a consumer's link binds nothing of its own to anira's copy of tanh-lib and nothing of anira's to the consumer's. That is exact for out-of-line code; tanh-lib's header-only code stays in weak COMDAT groups, which the final link merges with the host's instantiations of the same signature, so for that code the separation assumes the host uses the tanh-lib version anira pins. Mach-O, PE, a multi-config generator and a toolchain without `readelf`/`objcopy` keep a plain archive (the configure log names the reason), where the link order picks one tanh-lib copy for both (`docs/sphinx/troubleshooting.rst`, "A host that uses tanh-lib itself"). The engine archives are hidden per format by their `anira::<engine>` targets (`cmake/aniraEngineHelpers.cmake`: `--exclude-libs` on ELF, `-load_hidden` on Mach-O, nothing to hide on PE and Wasm), so the plugin's own `tanh_set_export_allowlist(<plugin> SYMBOL clap_entry)` is the only export decision left: `anira_*` never appears in a plugin's export table unless the plugin lists it. A shared consumer links `libanira.so.0` / `libanira.0.dylib` / `anira-0.lib` (`anira-0.dll`) through the alphas, major 3 from the freeze, and calls `anira_check_abi(ANIRA_ABI_VERSION)` first. There is no function table and no `anira_get_proc_address`: every entry is linked directly, a `dlopen` host resolves each name it uses with `dlsym`, and `anira.hpp` `#error`s on `ANIRA_CXX_MANUAL_INIT` and `ANIRA_NO_PROTOTYPES` (`anira.hpp:102-105`), so the driver thread pays no indirection.

### Versioning

Two integers own the promise, independent of the semver triple:

```c
/* abi/version.h (generated from abi/anira.yml by tools/abi/gen.py, like every abi/*.h; never hand-edited) */
#include <anira/abi/build_info.h>        /* ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR/MINOR and ANIRA_VERSION_*, configured from git by cmake/build-info.cmake */
#define ANIRA_MAKE_ABI_VERSION(major, minor) (((uint32_t)(major) << 16) | (uint32_t)(minor))
#define ANIRA_ABI_VERSION ANIRA_MAKE_ABI_VERSION(ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR, ANIRA_ABI_MINOR)
#define ANIRA_ABI_VERSION_MAJOR(v) ((uint32_t)(v) >> 16)
#define ANIRA_ABI_VERSION_MINOR(v) ((uint32_t)(v) & 0xffffu)
/* abi/build_info.h (configured by cmake/build-info.cmake from cmake/build_info.h.in and the git tag -- one source, the tag; as at v3.0.0-alpha.2) */
#define ANIRA_VERSION_MAJOR 3
#define ANIRA_VERSION_MINOR 0
#define ANIRA_VERSION_PATCH 0
#define ANIRA_VERSION_STRING "3.0.0-alpha.2"     /* git describe without the v; "3.0.0-alpha.2-12-gabc123" past the tag */
#define ANIRA_MAKE_VERSION(maj, min, pat) (((uint32_t)(maj) << 22) | ((uint32_t)(min) << 12) | (uint32_t)(pat))   /* Vulkan packing */
#define ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR 0                     /* 0 until v3.0.0, then the tag's major; SOVERSION; the MACHO versions' major; the DLL suffix */
#define ANIRA_ABI_MINOR 2                     /* 0.N for the N-th v3.0.0 pre-release, X.Y from vX.Y.Z, the next minor past a tag; never moves in a patch */

uint32_t     anira_abi_version(void);                         /* [thread-safe] NB; the library's ANIRA_ABI_VERSION */
anira_status anira_check_abi(uint32_t header_abi_version);    /* [thread-safe] NB; OK iff same major and lib minor >= header minor (exact while major 0) */
uint32_t     anira_version(void);                             /* [thread-safe] NB; ANIRA_MAKE_VERSION(...) of the library */
const char*  anira_version_string(void);                      /* [thread-safe] NB; git describe, static storage */
```

`anira_check_abi` is the one negotiation call; `ANIRA_ERROR_ABI_VERSION` otherwise. While the major is 0 it demands an exact match, so a binary built against alpha.1's headers (ABI 0.1) is refused by an alpha.2 library (0.2). `anira_version_string` replaces `anira_get_version()` of the 2.x web wrapper (`v2.3.0:src/emscripten-wrappers/InferenceConfig.cpp:28-30`). Static builds, the Wasm module and Windows have no soname, so a function-level negotiation exists beside it; packing `(major << 16) | minor` keeps the check one integer that TS compares without `BigInt`.

The promise inside ABI major 3, from v3.0.0: existing functions keep name, signature, semantics, thread tag and RT class; existing enum values and Tier-1 POD layouts never change; a minor may append functions, enum values (below `_FORCE32`), positive statuses, extension revisions, descriptor tail slots (after the last v3.0.0 slot) and new `anira_em_*` companions, each bumping `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR`; a patch changes nothing in the header. Deprecation is `ANIRA_DEPRECATED("use anira_x")` plus a `**Deprecated:**` CHANGELOG entry; the symbol stays exported until the next major. A major bump happens only for an RT POD layout change, a removed or renamed function, a changed enum value, or a changed thread or RT contract. Everything section 10 lists as deferred-additive fits inside major 3.

CMake: `cmake/build-info.cmake` derives `ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR`/`ANIRA_ABI_MINOR` from the git tag (`0.N` for the N-th `v3.0.0-*` pre-release reachable from HEAD, one running counter over alphas and betas; `X.Y` from `vX.Y.Z`; the next minor when the checkout is past a tag, so a development header never claims a released promise it exceeds; `0.0` without a reachable v3 tag) and writes them with the semver triple into the generated `abi/build_info.h` (`cmake/build_info.h.in`), which the generated `abi/version.h` includes -- the tag is the one source, nothing is bumped by hand, and the configure log names the origin (`ABI version: 0.2 (v3.0.0-alpha.2, pre-release 2 of the 3.0.0 line)`). The library is named after the ABI pair, not the release (`CMakeLists.txt:402-415`): `VERSION ${ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR}.${ANIRA_ABI_MINOR}`, `SOVERSION ${ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR}`, and `MACHO_CURRENT_VERSION` and `MACHO_COMPATIBILITY_VERSION` both `${ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR}.${ANIRA_ABI_MINOR}`, so dyld refuses a library older than the ABI minor a client linked; `OUTPUT_NAME anira-${ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR}` for a shared library when `TANH_BINARY_FORMAT STREQUAL "PE"`, where a DLL has no soname (a static PE archive keeps `anira.lib`). Through the alphas that is `libanira.so.0.2` with the soname `libanira.so.0`, `libanira.0.dylib`, and `anira-0.dll` with `anira-0.lib`; the major becomes 3 at the freeze. `aniraConfigVersion.cmake` is `COMPATIBILITY SameMajorVersion` (`cmake/install.cmake:393-397`). Semver major and ABI major are kept equal by policy from 3.0.0, so the Debian runtime package is `libanira3`; during the alphas that package wraps `libanira.so.0` and `find_package(anira 3.0)` accepts mutually incompatible alphas -- accepted, `anira_check_abi` is the gate that matters then. Prerelease tags: tanh-tooling's `tanh_git_version` (`cmake/tanh/git-version.cmake`, pinned v0.3.0) strips the pre-release identifier for `project()` and `write_basic_package_version_file` and reports it as `TANH_VERSION_PRERELEASE` beside `TANH_VERSION_DISTANCE`; anira calls it with `MATCH "v3*"` (`CMakeLists.txt:56`), so the 2.x tags the branch also reaches never name a build, and keeps the full describe string for `ANIRA_VERSION_STRING`. The 2.x cross-session version-string compare (`v2.3.0:src/scheduler/Context.cpp:325-348`) is dropped: two static embeddings have two cores and cannot see each other, and inside one core every session shares one header.

ABI 0 before the freeze: the header exists in full, `ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR` is 0, `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR` is the alpha/beta ordinal, `anira_check_abi` demands an exact match, gates 1, 3, 4, 5 and 6 fail the build, gate 2 arrives with the freeze and the registry diff of principle 8 reports, the CHANGELOG prefixes header changes with `**ABI (unstable):**`, npm publishes under `next`. The freeze is the `v3.0.0` tag, from which `cmake/build-info.cmake` derives `3.0`, on the commit that commits `abi/symbols-3.txt`, `abi/layout-3.txt` and `abi/anira-3.0.<arch>.abi`.

### The three object classes

| class | mechanism | why |
|---|---|---|
| Tier-1 RT PODs: `anira_tensor`, `anira_sync_token`, `anira_memory_handle`, `anira_processor_ctx`, `anira_engine_ctx`, `anira_log_record`, `anira_error` | fixed layout, no `struct_size`, pinned `sizeof`, versioned by ABI major only; a new shape is a new struct plus new functions | copied through lock-free FIFOs, read per block or per chunk, or value-initialised by `anira::Result<T>`; no size switch, no chain walk |
| config objects: `anira_context_config`, `anira_model_config`, `anira_tensor_spec`, `anira_contract`, `anira_job_options`, `anira_pipeline` | opaque handle + scalar setters/getters (ORT `SessionOptions` shape), all `[main-thread]`, all may allocate | the layout never enters the ABI; JS builds a config with one call per field instead of reproducing libc++ vector layout (`v2.3.0:web/src/wrappers/InferenceHandler.ts:201-212`) |
| Tier-2 descriptors handed once to a setter or filled by an enumerator: device blocks, `anira_log_desc`, `anira_processor_desc`, `anira_engine_desc`, `anira_backend_id`, `anira_plan_query`, extension payloads, `anira_edge_info`, `anira_plan_slot`, `anira_plan_ext`, `anira_plan_info`, and the records anira fills for a callback (`anira_init_info`, `anira_prepare_info`, `anira_engine_load_info`) | `uint32_t struct_size` first; descriptors with callbacks are `{struct_size, abi_version, user_data, ...slots}`; tail growth appends after the last v3.0.0 slot; the library reads or fills only inside `min(struct_size, sizeof(lib's))` | tail growth without `pNext`; `user_data`'s offset can never move; TS writes no field by hand |

Tier-1 layouts, identical on wasm32, LP64, LLP64, 32-bit ARM and 32-bit MSVC because every pointer sits in an `ANIRA_PTR` slot and every count is `uint32_t`/`uint64_t` at its natural alignment (verified with clang 22 and emcc 4.0.23); the i386 System V ABI, where 8-byte integers align to 4, is not a target:

| struct | `anira_struct_id` | bytes | align |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anira_tensor` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_TENSOR` (1) | 216 | 8 |
| `anira_sync_token` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_SYNC_TOKEN` (2) | 24 | 8 |
| `anira_memory_handle` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_MEMORY_HANDLE` (3) | 24 | 8 |
| `anira_processor_ctx` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_PROCESSOR_CTX` (4) | 64 | 8 |
| `anira_log_record` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_LOG_RECORD` (5) | 56 | 8 |
| `anira_error` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_ERROR` (6) | 520 | 4 |
| `anira_engine_ctx` | `ANIRA_STRUCT_ENGINE_CTX` (12) | 64 | 8 |

The table, with every `offsetof`, is `abi/layout-<major>.txt` (`layout-0.txt` through the pre-releases, `layout-3.txt` at the freeze), written by `tools/abi/gen.py` from the registry's natural-alignment model, with `union` blocks and dotted member paths, and verified by gate 3: the generated `test/abi/generated/test_layout.c` prints the table natively and under node and `cmake/abi-layout-check.cmake` diffs it against the committed file. While the major is 0 it grows with the pre-releases; from v3.0.0 it changes only in a commit that changes `ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR`. `anira_sizeof(anira_struct_id)` exists for allocators that cannot see the header; it answers every registered record id -- the seven Tier-1 ids (`ANIRA_STRUCT_TENSOR = 1` ... `ANIRA_STRUCT_ERROR = 6`, `ANIRA_STRUCT_ENGINE_CTX = 12`) and the enumerated Tier-2 records, `ANIRA_STRUCT_EDGE_INFO = 7`, `ANIRA_STRUCT_PLAN_SLOT = 8`, `ANIRA_STRUCT_PLAN_EXT = 9`, `ANIRA_STRUCT_PLAN_INFO = 10`, `ANIRA_STRUCT_BACKEND_ID = 11` and `ANIRA_STRUCT_PLAN_QUERY = 13` -- and returns 0 for an unknown one, which is how TS gets the stride for `anira_capabilities_edges`, `anira_capabilities_backends`, `anira_enabled_engines`, `anira_pipeline_capabilities_backends`, `anira_plan_report_plans`, `anira_plan_report_slots` and `anira_plan_report_exts` without computing a descriptor layout by hand; ids `0x0001xxxx` are reserved for extension payloads, `0x0004xxxx` for Emscripten-only structs. `abi_version` in a descriptor is the packed `ANIRA_ABI_VERSION` the host compiled against, so anira can gate calls into an older host. Descriptors are target-layout (they hold pointers) and are handed to a setter once, never read per block; TS never writes one, it uses the setters, the `_set_ext_json` twins and the `anira_em_*_init` fills.

Enumeration convention: array enumerators take the caller's element stride explicitly -- `(uint32_t element_size, uint32_t* count, T* out)`, `element_size = sizeof(T)` in C, `anira_sizeof(id)` from TS -- and write `min(element_size, sizeof(lib's T))` bytes per element at that stride (`anira_capabilities_edges`, `anira_capabilities_backends`, `anira_enabled_engines`, `anira_pipeline_capabilities_backends`, `anira_plan_report_plans`, `anira_plan_report_slots`, `anira_plan_report_exts` -- a backend is an engine paired with a provider, so it enumerates as a record like the rest); scalar enumerators (`anira_capabilities_domains/ext_kinds`, `anira_registered_ext_kinds`, `anira_handler_get_latencies`) are `(uint32_t* count, T* out)` without a stride. `out == NULL` returns the count; a short buffer fills what fits and returns `ANIRA_INCOMPLETE`. The explicit stride is what makes `struct_size` growth usable for arrays without per-element pre-initialisation.

### Error model

```c
typedef enum anira_status {
    ANIRA_OK = 0, ANIRA_SUCCESS_UPGRADED = 1 /* v2 JSON auto-upgraded */, ANIRA_INCOMPLETE = 2 /* enumeration buffer short */,
    ANIRA_TIMEOUT = 3, ANIRA_PENDING = 4, ANIRA_MISSED = 5 /* a Hard entry delivered the miss policy's fill, also a _wait twin at its timeout; not recorded in rt_error */,
    ANIRA_ERROR_UNKNOWN = -1, ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_ARGUMENT = -2, ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_STATE = -3, ANIRA_ERROR_OUT_OF_MEMORY = -4,
    ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED = -5 /* engine, provider, domain or kind not available here */, ANIRA_ERROR_NO_SUCH_FILE = -6, ANIRA_ERROR_MODEL_LOAD = -7,
    ANIRA_ERROR_ENGINE = -8, ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG = -9 /* prepare-time legality (section 2), and the real-time refusals recorded in anira_handler_rt_error: a tensor's dtype against its slot or spec, a ring or tensor accessor's dtype, a processor's hop count */, ANIRA_ERROR_EXTENSION_UNCONSUMED = -10,
    ANIRA_ERROR_EXTENSION_UNKNOWN = -11, ANIRA_ERROR_EDGE_UNREACHABLE = -12, ANIRA_ERROR_BUDGET = -13 /* Hard validation */,
    ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY = -14 /* RT: no free slot / ring full */, ANIRA_ERROR_TICKET_STALE = -15, ANIRA_ERROR_WRONG_CONTRACT = -16,
    ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_PREPARED = -17, ANIRA_ERROR_JSON = -18, ANIRA_ERROR_ABI_VERSION = -19, ANIRA_ERROR_BUFFER_TOO_SMALL = -20,
    ANIRA_ERROR_DEVICE = -21, ANIRA_ERROR_EXTENSION_VERSION = -22, ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_FOUND = -23 /* anira_plan_report_find: no plan matches */,
    ANIRA_ERROR_INTERNAL = -100 /* an exception the firewall could not classify: a bug in anira */, ANIRA_STATUS_FORCE32 = 0x7fffffff
} anira_status;
#define ANIRA_FAILED(s)    ((int32_t)(s) < 0)          /* the only stable failure test */
#define ANIRA_SUCCEEDED(s) ((int32_t)(s) >= 0)
#define ANIRA_ERROR_MESSAGE_CAPACITY 512
typedef struct anira_error { int32_t status; uint32_t reserved; char message[ANIRA_ERROR_MESSAGE_CAPACITY]; } anira_error;   /* Tier 1, 520 bytes, frozen */
#define ANIRA_ERROR_INIT ANIRA_INIT(anira_error, ANIRA_OK, 0u, {0})
```

Every fallible function returns `anira_status`; created handles come back through an `out` parameter; functions that can produce a message take `anira_error* err` (nullable) as their last parameter and fill it on failure, out-parameters untouched. Positive statuses are informational successes -- `ANIRA_SUCCESS_UPGRADED` lets TS surface the one-time v2-JSON warning, `ANIRA_INCOMPLETE` serves the enumeration convention, `ANIRA_MISSED` says a Hard entry delivered the miss policy's fill -- and the set a function may return is not frozen: a minor may add one, which is why the stable idiom is `ANIRA_FAILED(s)` and never `s != ANIRA_OK`; the section 9 sketches, `anira.hpp` and the generated TS bindings (whose `checkStatus` in `web/src/abi/bindings.ts` throws an `AniraError` on a negative status) all use it. `anira_status_string(status)` returns static text and is tagged `[thread-safe]` only: not `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`, and not `[callback-safe]` because that list is closed.
