The `allow_smaller_buffers` sweep counts down from the greatest relative buffer size with a float loop counter, `while (--greatest_buffer_size > 0)` (`:315`), recomputing latencies and struct counts on every step: millions of iterations when that size is large, and above 2^24 a decrement of one is no longer representable, so the counter can round back to its own value and the sweep stops making progress. It becomes a closed form over the same quantity. v3 drops `allow_smaller_buffers` for a declared block range (section 3), so that fix matters only for the 2.x line, while the overflow travels into v3 with the ring sizing M2 carries across. Neither defect is reachable from an Async, Buffer-role video path: a Buffer-role tensor has no hop, both sweeps skip it, and Async sizes no ring.
6. `feat/offline-inference` does **not** ship: it folds into `Async` in M3 (section 10).

Items 3 and 4 touch `main` only and merge into `v3` from there; the tag waits for neither. The stable, versioned C ABI itself is not an M0 deliverable: it is the spine of M1-M6 and freezes in M6 (section 6a, and the ABI rows of every milestone below); all M0 carries of it is the `unloadtest_*` rename it makes necessary. Effort beyond the tag itself: 0.1 person-days.

Exit: tag `v2.3.0`, release artifacts from `on_tag.yml`, npm 2.3.0 from `publish_web.yml`. "v2 parity" everywhere in this document means this tag.

### M1 -- configuration layer (`v3` branch; no runtime change; target 07.09.2026)

Everything in sections 1b, 2, 3, 4 (the struct), 5, 6 (`edge_cost`) and 8 that is data, spelled as C handles with scalar setters: `anira_tensor_spec` with tagged axes, roles, window/context/latency; `anira_model_config` with models by index, the per-entry tensor record (`set_tensor_name`, `set_tensor_layout`), `set_model_bytes`, `set_anchor` by canonical name; `anira_context_config` with the device descriptors and the log scalars; `anira_contract` as the tagged handle behind `anira::Contract = std::variant<Hard, Async>`, `hard_set_wait_ratio` included; `anira_job_options` with its frame-invariant setters; the extension registry keyed `(kind, version)` with `anira_ext_entry` as its one row, `anira_registered_ext_kinds`, the consumed-or-fail walk; `anira_model_config_from_json`/`anira_context_config_from_json`/`anira_contract_from_json` with the v2 auto-upgrade (`ANIRA_SUCCESS_UPGRADED`, one warning per process, `take_legacy_contract`). Internally a translator from the new handles to v2's `InferenceConfig`/`ContextConfig`/`HostConfig`, so the v2 engine runs unchanged behind the v3 configuration; the v2 classes stay public and exported through this milestone, `include/anira/anira.h` stays the v2 C++ umbrella, and `USE_*`/`ANIRA_VERSION` stay PUBLIC because the v2 headers still need them. The deprecated aliases (`InferenceConfig`, `ContextConfig`, `Context`, `JsonConfigLoader`) are not a v3 deliverable; they become the constructor shims of `compat/v2.hpp` in M2.

ABI work in this milestone:

- **First PR**: the `v3` CI branch filters (above) and `cmake/build-info.cmake` over tanh-tooling v0.2.7's prerelease-aware `tanh_git_version` (`MATCH "v3*"`; so `v3.0.0-alpha.1` configures and the full describe string stays `ANIRA_VERSION_STRING`), which also derives `ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR`/`MINOR` from the tag into the generated `abi/build_info.h` -- before the first alpha tag.
- **Frozen at M1**, included per file (no C umbrella yet): `abi/export.h`, `abi/version.h`, `abi/build_info.h` (generated), `abi/status.h`, `abi/enums.h` with every value pinned: `anira_engine` and `anira_provider` as the two independent axes, and every domain and sync arm M4-M6 will populate; `abi/config.h`, `abi/log.h`.
- **Implementation**: `src/capi/{version,error,config}.cpp` with the exception firewall; `tanh_set_export_allowlist(anira NAMESPACE anira SYMBOL "anira_*")` and the export check's `ALLOW_REGEX "^_?anira_[a-z0-9_]+$"`.
- **Tooling**: the registry `abi/anira.yml` and its generator `tools/abi/gen.py`, which emit the C headers themselves (never hand-edited; the Vulkan-Headers pattern), `web/src/abi/enums.ts`, `exports_wasm.txt`, both symbol lists, `src/capi/generated/status_strings.inc` and `test/abi/generated/test_layout.c` (the proc table behind `anira_get_proc_address` this item planned was never generated, and the entry is cut), and validate thread tags, the 64-bit rule and the descriptor field order while doing so; `abi/layout-0.txt` committed; `gen.py --diff-against <tag>` for the release-time classification of principle 8.
- **Tests**: the prepare-time legality rules of section 2, JSON round trips, the v2 upgrade, consumed-or-fail by name, `test/abi/generated/test_layout.c` and `test/abi/header_c.c`.
- **Gates online**: 3 (layout table, native), 4 (per-file C11 and C++17 compile; the strict-isolation half and the Wasm arm follow the umbrella at M2) and the registry check `anira_abi_generate`. Effort beyond the configuration layer itself: 2.0 person-days.
- **As built** (`v3.0.0-alpha.1`, ABI 0.1, 2026-09-03; PRs #159-#169 on `v3`): everything above, plus two items pulled forward from M2 because they depend on the C handles alone -- the configuration half of `anira.hpp` (C++20, header-only, not ABI-stable) and a transitional bridge, `anira/compat/v3_to_v2.h` (`namespace anira::v3compat`: `to_inference_config(model, contract, candidates)`, `to_context_config(context)`, `to_host_config(contract, model)`, `enabled_engines()`), which the examples use until the 3.x handler lands and which is removed with it; the per-entry tensor record (`models[].tensors`: the export's name and the axis `layout`, section 5) instead of names alone; `anira_contract_hard_set_ring_dtype` (data only; the bridge takes float32). The bundled models ship as configuration files (`extras/models/**/*.model.json`, `*.contract.json`, named in `extras/models/model_files.h`), every example loads them the same way, and the 2.x fixture headers are gone; the model tree is addressed from the one root `ANIRA_EXTRAS_MODELS_DIR`.

Why first: driver-free, the largest public surface, and it makes every later milestone testable through the final API.

Exit: every example builds against the v3 configuration types; `v3.0.0-alpha.1`.

### M2 -- Tensor and the Host-only pump (target 07.09.2026)

`anira_tensor`, `anira_sync_token`, `anira_memory_handle` as the frozen 216/24/24-byte PODs, with planar host channels (`ANIRA_TENSOR_PLANAR`); the engines and the accessors read `ANIRA_DOMAIN_HOST` and `ANIRA_DOMAIN_HOST_PINNED` alone, and the six GPU factories and the four draft ones have field-fill bodies (the ledger of `CHANGELOG.md:42`); the edge registry, `anira_plan_report` and cost classes with Host rows only; `anira_context` as a refcounted handle over the immortal core, the private `anira::Core` (`src/scheduler/Core.h`) -- thread pool core-owned, the first context sizes it, later contexts reconcile per field, `anira_context_destroy` joins nothing; `anira_handler_create(context, pipeline)` over `anira_pipeline_add_inference`, `anira_pipeline_add_processor` (at most one processor) and `anira_pipeline_add_engine`, and `anira_handler_prepare(h, hard)` compiling one plan per model entry and candidate over the RingChunker, which is v2's ring buffers moved off the `float` instantiation 2.x used (`v2.3.0:include/anira/utils/RingBuffer.h:39`; the send and receive vectors of `v2.3.0:include/anira/scheduler/SessionElement.h:156-157`) and onto the type parameter through `RingBufferT<T>` (`src/utils/RingBuffer.h`), `T` the element type of the host stream on that slot -- what the driver pushes into an input ring, what the driver pops out of an output ring -- which is the ring dtype the host declared for the slot on the Hard contract (`anira_contract_hard_set_ring_dtype`, `ANIRA_DTYPE_F32` when unset), instantiated per slot at `prepare`; crossing only as `anira_ring*` with the typed block accessors; `anira_engine_desc` and the pinned `anira_engine` and `anira_provider` values replacing the five `#ifdef` paths of 2.x (`InferenceBackend` members, `SessionElement`'s per-backend pointers, `InferenceThread::inference`) with the adapters of `src/engines/`, so nothing in the header depends on which engines a build enabled and presence is `anira_enabled_engines`; the shared engine environments of section 4 (one environment per engine object, one intra-op thread per ONNX Runtime session, `src/engines/OnnxRuntimeAdapter.cpp:205-278`); `anira::v2::LegacyProcessor` around existing `PrePostProcessor` subclasses; the Static store (`anira_handler_set_static_input` / `anira_handler_get_static_output`, whole tensors) materialised by the handler before `pre_process` and after `post_process`; declared State pairs, bound and flipped by anira on the inference thread; the LibTorch and ExecuTorch adapters consuming `anira_ext_entry`; the core owning the real-time log drain, context-scoped C sinks behind one trampoline on the private `thl::Logger`.

ABI work in this milestone, the largest of the roadmap:

- **Frozen at M2**: `abi/tensor.h` (with `ANIRA_TENSOR_PLANAR`, `anira_tensor_init_host_planar` and `anira_sync_token_dup` / `_reset`), `abi/context.h`, `abi/core.h`, `abi/thread.h` (with `has_exited`), `abi/lifecycle.h`, `abi/processor.h` with the 64-byte `anira_processor_ctx` and `abi/engine.h` with the 64-byte `anira_engine_ctx` (`backend` became `engine` plus `provider`; `abi/layout-0.txt` records both at 64 bytes), and `abi/handler.h` with the tensor entries alone -- `anira_handler_process` / `process_multi` / `push_data` / `push_data_multi` / `pop_data` / `pop_data_multi` over one `const anira_tensor*` per slot, each tensor in its slot's ring dtype (`anira_contract_hard_set_ring_dtype`, on `abi/config.h` since the alpha.1 tag), and the `_wait` twins of the process and pop forms (section 6) -- the dense plan index with `anira_handler_set_plan` / `anira_handler_get_plan` as the entire selection surface and `anira_plan_report_find` beside it, and the Static pair `anira_handler_set_static_input` / `anira_status anira_handler_get_static_output(anira_handler*, uint32_t slot, const anira_tensor* out)`, whole tensors in the spec's dtype (non-const: a refusal is recorded), because a per-element read has no snapshot and a classifier vector read across a frame boundary could mix two inferences where the whole-tensor read is one. There is no `_typed`, `_f32` or `_separate` entry and no scalar Static entry. `include/anira/anira.h` is the C11 umbrella and `anira_all.h` adds `abi/draft/` and `anira_em.h`; `abi/draft/tensor_platform.h` declares the unmeasured arms under `abi/symbols-draft.txt`.
- **Implementation**: `src/capi/{context,core,thread,handler,processor,engine,tensor}.cpp` with the per-chunk fill of `anira_processor_ctx` and `anira_engine_ctx`, the Static store, the declared State pairs and refcounted carriers behind every descriptor, engine object and `BORROW`ed model bytes; capabilities; user-driven inference threads; the `anira_shutdown` family retargeted from `Core::shutdown()`; the internal `ANIRA_REALTIME` replaced by `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` so the public and the internal attribute are one definition. Since PR 10b (#227) the core runs on the 3.x types: the session reads the model config, the contract snapshot and `capi::Derived` directly (`v3_to_v2` is gone), `capi::ProcessorRunner` (`src/capi/processor.h`) runs the processor's phases on the chunk, each Streamed slot's ring lives in its `capi::StreamPort` (`src/capi/port.h:314`), and the chunk storage is anira's own (one allocation per chunk, every slot 64-byte aligned and typed, no buffer for State). The overload contract: with no free inference slot, every ready hop of a call is dropped and filled once under `on_miss` at its stream position, `anira_handler_rt_error` records `ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY`, and the catch-up never delivers a late sample. `tools/abi/gen.py` also writes `web/src/abi/layout.ts` and `bindings.ts`, `abi/layout-0.txt`, `abi/symbols-emscripten.txt`, `src/capi/generated/struct_sizes.inc` and `test/abi/generated/link_probe.c`.
- **Build**: the core's C++ headers private under `src/` (`scheduler/`, `utils/`, `engines/`, `capi/`) and never installed, no C++ class or function carrying `ANIRA_API` (`system/Exports.h` and the 2.x export headers deleted); `anira_objects`, the OBJECT library the library and the white-box suites link; tanh-lib (pinned at 7ae7c69, v0.4.0 with tanh-tooling v0.3.0), concurrentqueue and nlohmann_json absorbed and private, a parent's `tanh_core` accepted only as a static archive with `TANH_STATIC` (a configure error otherwise) and a compiled log ceiling other than `TANH_LOG_COMPILED_MAX_LEVEL 4` warned about (`CMakeLists.txt:229-264`); a static ELF libanira is one prelinked object with only `anira_*` global (`cmake/prelink-archive.cmake`), a plain archive on Mach-O, PE, multi-config generators and toolchains without `readelf`/`objcopy`; the benchmark fixture moved to `examples/benchmark/fixture/` (`anira_benchmark_fixture`, static, not installed), no test library linked into `anira`, the explicit install list of `cmake/public-headers.cmake`, `Config.cmake.in` with `find_dependency(Threads)` alone; `USE_*` / `ENABLE_LOGGING` / `ANIRA_VERSION` PUBLIC on `anira_objects` and on `anira` through `$<BUILD_INTERFACE:>` only (`CMakeLists.txt:532-535`), `TORCH_CXX_FLAGS` on `anira_objects` alone; the allowlist `SYMBOL "anira_*"` (`CMakeLists.txt:420`) with gate 1 strict; `VERSION`, `SOVERSION`, `MACHO_*` and `OUTPUT_NAME` from the tag-derived ABI pair of `cmake/build-info.cmake` (`CMakeLists.txt:408-415`): `libanira.so.0`, `libanira.0.dylib` and `anira-0.dll` through the pre-releases.
- **Web**: `src/emscripten-wrappers/` deleted; the generated `anira/anira_em.h` (`ANIRA_EM_HOOKS_VERSION 2`) over `src/platform/emscripten/{em_hooks,em_worker,em_audio,em_runtime,em_console}.cpp`; one module on one shared memory, linked with `-pthread -sWASM_WORKERS -sAUDIO_WORKLET` on emsdk 4.0.23 against the generated `web/src/abi/exports_wasm.txt` (`cmake/build-wasm.cmake`), exception catching kept, 256 MiB growing to 2 GiB; the context's inference threads are Wasm Workers anira starts from C, each importing the context's worker module (`anira_em_context_config_set_worker_module`) before its loop, and a handler streams through a Wasm Audio Worklet node written in C (`anira_em_audio_node_*`), whose render callback makes one `anira_handler_process_multi` call per quantum over planar tensors; JavaScript processors and engines through the per-scope registry (`defineProcessor` / `defineEngine`, `anira_em_pipeline_add_js_processor` / `anira_em_pipeline_add_js_engine`), ONNX Runtime Web one such engine; the typed TS package over the generated `web/src/abi/bindings.ts`, `enums.ts` and `layout.ts`, its names those of `anira.hpp` (section 6b). One addition here that is not ABI work and pays for itself immediately: the Wasm build has no blocking queue (`WaitStrategy::Blocking` is coerced away), so `run_loop`'s backoff would spin a core per idle inference Worker for the whole session; an atomic wake word with `memory.atomic.notify` at the two enqueue sites lets it park instead -- invisible to the ABI, a win in every browser today, and the primitive any later externally-driven pump needs.
- **Wrappers and tests**: `anira.hpp` (the configuration, the pipeline -- `add_inference` / `add_processor` / `add_engine` with `anira::Processor` and `anira::Engine` -- and the handler half: `InferenceHandler` over `Tensor` with the `_wait` twins, the Static pair and `PlanReport::find`) and `compat/v2.hpp` (constructor shims, `PrePostProcessor` views re-pointed per call, `LegacyProcessor`, the 2.x `InferenceHandler` over the C handler, `PassthroughEngine` and the 2.x base backend as an `anira::Engine` under `anira.v2.custom`, `set_non_realtime` routing); the 2.x `InferenceHandler` and the alpha.1 bridge deleted (#225), then every 2.x class nothing used (#227); the v2 suite run through them, and the whole suite under RTSan; JUCE and CLAP examples on `anira.hpp`; the `test/contracts/unload` death test retargeted at the C ABI; `test/contracts/install/` with `consumer_c` (`main_c.c`: an engine with `ANIRA_ENGINE_FLAG_NEEDS_NO_MODEL`, an EXPLICIT budget and a FIXED warm-up) and `consumer_tanh_first` (`main_tanh_first.cpp`). One rule splits the suite: a black-box binary links `anira::anira` and sees what a consumer sees, the public headers and the library's exports (`test_handler`, the header gates, `anira_abi_link`, the install consumers, the unload module, the examples); a `WHITE_BOX` binary reads anira's private classes, so it links the library's objects (`anira_objects`) with `src/` on its include path and `ANIRA_STATIC` defined (`test_utils`, `test_scheduler`, `test_engines`, `test_abi`; `anira_add_test_binary`, `test/CMakeLists.txt:63-79`). The partition of the cut-over (`test/CMakeLists.txt:114-135`) moves every TU once: (a) through `compat/v2.hpp`, `test_InferenceHandlerApi` and `test_OneSidedStreaming`; (b) rewritten on `anira.hpp`, `test_InferenceHandler`; (c) white-box, `test_scheduler`, `test_engines`, `test_utils`, `test_OneSidedStreamingInternals` and `test_abi`; (d) retired with their subject, `test_Bridge`, `test_HighPriorityThread`, `test_JsonConfigLoader`, `test_CoreConfig`, `test_InferenceConfig`, `test_PrePostProcessor`, `test_Buffer` and `test_HelperFunctions`, with `test_Translate` now `test_Validate`; (e) `test_BackendLinkage` is `test/contracts/test_EngineLinkage.cpp`, over `anira_enabled_engines`.
- **Gates online**: 1 (symbol baselines with the draft list, strict since PR 12: nothing beside the two lists; `anira_abi_link` on every native preset; on Wasm the `-sEXPORTED_FUNCTIONS` link and the `.d.ts` export diff), 3 under node (`anira_abi_wasm_gates`, against the `abi/layout-0.txt` that gen.py writes beside `layout.ts`), 4 strict on every preset (the umbrellas, `anira.hpp`, `compat/v2.hpp` and the isolation probes), 5, 6 (the whole suite under RTSan, `-Werror=function-effects` on the consumer-shaped targets). Effort beyond the pump work: 5.0 person-days.
- **As built** (revised 2026-11-01): PR 1-3 (#171-#173: typed rings, the context over the immortal core, `abi/handler.h`), PR 5 with 5b-1 and 5b-2 (#187-#190: `abi/tensor.h` and the tensor forms of the Hard entries), PR 6 (#191: the stage descriptor, the processor since PR 10; the Static store; declared state passing), PR 7 (#193: the engine descriptor, the plan table, binding by name), PR 8a (#195: the read-back getters) and PR 8b (#196: `compat/v2.hpp`), the runtime findings of PR 8 (#197: instance-aware dispatch, `ANIRA_INSTANCES_AUTO`) and fractional Hard blocks with the anchor hop (#198); then the six PRs that end M2. PR 9 (#210), the web hooks: `anira_em.h` and `em_hooks.cpp`, `src/emscripten-wrappers/` and the 2.x TS classes deleted, gates 3 and 4 under node, headless Chromium and Firefox tests in `build_web`. PR 11 (#213, with the Debug-build memory fix #221), the lean web runtime, brought forward from M5 (it depends on PR 9 only): Emscripten's Wasm Workers and Wasm Audio Worklets (linked with `-pthread`, `-sWASM_WORKERS` and `-sAUDIO_WORKLET` on emsdk 4.0.23) in place of the hand-rolled runtime, the inference threads started from C, the audio node written in C, the memory growing from 256 MiB to 2 GiB, the TypeScript bindings generated from `abi/anira.yml` and the typed package mirroring `anira.hpp`, and one model and contract configuration for native and web (the context configuration may differ), so that users install from npm and write their own processors and engines in TS without rebuilding the wasm. PR 10 (#225), the runtime cut-over: the `anira.hpp` handler half, `<anira/anira.h>` as the C11 umbrella, the 2.x `InferenceHandler` and the alpha.1 bridge deleted, the stage become the processor (`abi/processor.h`, at most one per pipeline; `Pipeline` with `add_inference` / `add_processor` / `add_engine`), the engine object owning its id, `anira_plan_report_find` with `ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_FOUND`. PR 10b (#227): the core on the 3.x types natively, the internal `v3_to_v2` translation and the 2.x classes nothing used deleted, the overload contract (every ready hop of a call dropped and filled once under `on_miss`, `rt_error` CAPACITY, a strict catch-up) and the `host_` names of the Hard contract (`host_block_min` / `host_block_max` / `host_rate`, `anira_contract_hard_set_host_blocks`). PR 12 (#229), the build gates: the core's C++ headers private, libanira exporting `anira_*` alone (gate 1 strict), tanh-lib, concurrentqueue and nlohmann_json absorbed, the soname from the ABI pair (`libanira.so.0`), the explicit install list, gate 4 strict on every preset, gates 5 and 6, the prelinked static ELF libanira; it no longer depends on PR 4, since `consumer_c` spells an EXPLICIT budget and a FIXED warm-up. PR 13: these doc amendments, the registry diff and the `alpha.2` tag. PR 4 (MEASURED/UNTIL_STABLE) left M2 for M2b, and PR 4b and PR 7b, which `alpha.2` never required, moved with it; the Hard defaults therefore stay refused at `alpha.2` as at `alpha.1`.

Exit: the complete v2 test suite passes through the v3 API on the Hard path via `anira.hpp`/`compat/v2.hpp`; the JUCE and CLAP examples build on `anira.hpp`; `src/emscripten-wrappers/` is gone and the TS core runs over the C ABI; `consumer_c` runs the raw-C sketch of section 9 against the installed package, shared and static; every input and output ring in the pipeline is an instantiation of `RingBufferT<T>` and `anira_ring_dtype` reports the element type that ring actually holds; `v3.0.0-alpha.2`.

### M2b -- the Hard contract completed, and model variants (added 2026-10-29)

After `v3.0.0-alpha.2` and before M3 (Fares, 2026-10-29). It is named M2b so that M3-M6 keep their numbers and every reference to them stays valid. Four PRs, in this order, each on the one before:

1. **PR 4 -- measured and until-stable budgets.** The warm-up pass moves to prepare on the C path and runs through the adapters' instance pool (FIXED n and the MEASURED pass both at `anira_handler_prepare`); `ANIRA_BUDGET_MEASURED` is the largest inference time observed over that pass, `ANIRA_WARMUP_UNTIL_STABLE` iterates until the last four of at most 32 iterations lie within 20 % of their median (the M2 plan's criterion), and `ANIRA_ERROR_BUDGET` is returned when the latency schedule does not fit the struct pool. The last prepare refusals are lifted, so the defaults of `anira_contract_create_hard` prepare and the section-9 sketch runs unamended. Declared state is never fed from the session's buffers during the pass.
2. **PR 4b -- inline execution.** A Hard contract option `execution` (`worker`, `inline`, `auto`), chosen per plan at prepare and fixed until the next prepare: inline runs the inference on the driving thread inside the Hard entry, removing the inference term from the reported latency; `inline_ratio` bounds the share of the block period the inference may occupy, `auto` decides from PR 4's measured budget and chooses inline only for an engine that sets `ANIRA_ENGINE_FLAG_REALTIME_SAFE`, and an infeasible `inline` is `ANIRA_ERROR_BUDGET`; the plan report names the mode.
3. **PR 7b -- State initial values.** A reset re-initialises a declared state pair to its initial value: a host preset set at run time (`anira_handler_set_state_init`, sticky), else the model config's `state_init` record (the raw bytes of the tensor), else zeros; `anira_handler_get_state` snapshots the state while no inference of the session is in flight. The same value is what a stateful variant switch returns to.
4. **PR V -- the variant axis** (moved here from M5): one plan per (variant, candidate), that is per (variant, engine, provider) that has a model, which is when `anira_plan_report_plans` starts returning more than one row; per-plan Hard warm-up and budget, with the reported latency and the budget the worst case across the enabled set; `anira_handler_set_plan` stays one store of the plan index, `thl::RCU` (namespace `thl`) natively with readers registered at `prepare`, the relaxed atomic on Wasm in the plan of record, whose reason -- RCU's `thread_local` reader state could not exist there -- lapsed with PR 11, which gives every Wasm Worker and the worklet a thread-local block of their own, so PR V may use RCU on Wasm too; a stateful switch clears stream state at the next Hard entry or job boundary on the driving thread; `set_model(variant)` and `set_backend(engine, provider)` as the `anira.hpp` conveniences; validation at prepare that the variants agree on the external I/O.

ABI work: additive only on the surface that froze at M2; no frozen record changes. PR 4 changes no registry entry; PR 4b appends `anira_hard_execution`, `anira_contract_hard_set_execution`, `anira_contract_hard_set_inline_ratio` and the tail field `execution` of `anira_plan_info`; PR 7b appends `anira_handler_set_state_init`, `anira_handler_get_state` and the `state_init` extension kind; PR V moves no signature, because `variant` is already a field of `anira_plan_info` and the dense plan index froze with `abi/handler.h`. Effort, from the M2 plan: about 0.75 days for PR 4, 2 for PR 4b and 1.75 for PR 7b; PR V is not yet estimated.

Exit: the four PRs merged; `v3.0.0-alpha.3`.

### M3 -- Async (target 07.09.2026)

`anira_handler_submit`/`anira_ticket` (`ticket_status`, `ticket_wait`, `poll`, `ticket_input_released`, `ticket_output_ready`, `ticket_error`, `ticket_user_data`, `ticket_release`), `anira_job_options` gains `set_on_complete` (M1 shipped the handle and its frame-invariant setters; the per-job deadline is the `deadline_ms` argument of `submit`), `ViewChunker` and `IdentityChunker`, lanes, `max_in_flight`, EDF ordering and `ANIRA_LATE_DROP`, `ANIRA_DELIVERY_IMMEDIATE` with `anira_job_complete_fn`, `anira_handler_bind_output`, borrowed `from_host` lifetimes with one release per submit, the submit path writing `anira_processor_ctx.ticket` on every phase of the job it submitted (the field froze with `abi/processor.h` at M2; the Hard pump writes `ANIRA_TICKET_INVALID`); `feat/offline-inference` absorbed (lanes survive, tickets subsume callback + poll). Still Host-only. The stale-output check (section 7, completion) enters the test harness here, not in M4. The streaming-direction precondition is met (open decisions below).

ABI work:

- **Frozen at M3**: `abi/ticket.h` -- tickets as `uint32_t` slot|generation with `ANIRA_TICKET_INVALID = 0` and `ANIRA_ERROR_CAPACITY` after `lanes * max_in_flight + 8` unreleased tickets, `submit` moving owning `acquire` tokens into the slot and carrying its per-job `void* job_user_data` back out through `anira_handler_ticket_user_data`, `allocate_input`/`allocate_output`/`free_tensor` for Host, `anira_em_job_options_set_on_complete_js` (`anira_sync_token_dup` and `anira_sync_token_reset` froze in `abi/tensor.h` at M2).
- **Wrappers**: the Async half of `anira.hpp` (`Ticket` releasing its slot in the destructor, `submit` over `std::span<Tensor>`, `Ticket::user_data()`); TS `Ticket` (`userData` reading back the per-job number `submit` was given) and the host `Tensor`, completion forwarded from the Worker by `postMessage`.
- **Gates**: none new; the registry diff (`gen.py --diff-against`) classifies the appended `abi/ticket.h` names as a minor-or-pre-release change and the tag-derived `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR` moves with the tag -- the first exercise of the append rule. Effort beyond the Async work: 1.5 person-days.
- **Asynchronous engines on the web**: an inference Worker that leaves its blocking loop (`Atomics.waitAsync`, with a fallback where it is missing) so that an engine whose inference is a promise (onnxruntime-web `session.run()`, a WebGPU readback) completes the job's ticket later; today `process` is synchronous, unchanged since 2.x (`docs/sphinx/web-api/architecture.rst`, "What an Inference Worker and the Worklet Can See").

Exit: offline file rendering matches v2's non-real-time output; met/late/dropped accounting tested; `v3.0.0-alpha.4` (revised 2026-10-29: `alpha.3` is now M2b's exit).

M1-M3 carry 8.5 of the roadmap's 12.5 ABI person-days inside a nine-day window that already holds the configuration layer, the pump and Async. M2 and M3 are therefore the milestones most likely to slip past 07.09; that is accepted rather than thinning M2, which carries the freeze-relevant half, and the cut order below is what gives if the date does not. (Revised 2026-10-29: both slipped. M2 keeps its freeze-relevant half and ends at `alpha.2`; PR 4, PR 4b and PR 7b moved to M2b together with the variant axis, and the TS package rewrite moved from M5 into M2 as PR 11.)

### M4 -- Context probing and the Linux GPU domains (target 25.09.2026)

The prototype's `infer/` layer ported into the core and the context's probe, file by file: `infer_ctx_{vk,gl,wgpu,cuda}.c` -> the device blocks, the identity checks and the functional probes (section 4, three rungs, cached on the box); `infer_edge.c` and `infer_edge_cache` -> the registry and the plan-owned edge cache; `infer_vk_cuda.c` -> the opaque-fd rows and the three-hop bridge; `infer_engine_ort.c` -> the WebGPU and CUDA EP adapters (IOBinding, `user_compute_stream`, the EP-allocator device-id assertion, capture as a per-plan mode gated by the stale check, `ANIRA_SYNC_QUEUE_ORDERED`); `allocate_input`/`allocate_output` for the new domains; sync-token fd ownership (move at `submit`, deferred close by the inference thread); the byte image and `anira_context_byte_image_bytes`. Order by what is measured: `WgpuBuffer`/WebGPU EP on Mesa first, then the Vulkan, dma-buf and GL export rows, then CUDA on the NVIDIA box. Build: `build-deps.sh` becomes the external-Dawn ORT variant of the anira-project/backends release, with the Dawn revision assertion of section 4. Tests: `hello_inference`'s matrix becomes the functional-probe suite under ctest, `--strict` = `ANIRA_EDGE_COST_STRICT`. This milestone needs a GPU CI runner (Mesa and NVIDIA), which anira's CI does not have.

The emsdk 6.x move at the end of M4 (added 2026-10-29, Fares). Until then anira stays on emsdk 4.0.23, M2's pin, which already has `-pthread`, Wasm Workers and Wasm Audio Worklets. The prebuilt ONNX Runtime WASM archive must be rebuilt with 6.x first (anira-project/backends#24): it is LTO bitcode of 4.0.23's libc++, which is ABI-incompatible with 6.0.13's, so every model load of the built-in ONNX Runtime engine on the web fails at prepare. The move also handles `wasmBinary`/`wasmMemory` named in `INCOMING_MODULE_JS_API` (6.0.2), `MAXIMUM_MEMORY` enforced with an imported memory (6.0.12) and the rejected `MiB` suffix, and measures code size and speed, since no benefit is measured yet.

ABI work: additive only. The adapters and the validation behind `anira_tensor_init_cuda/gl_buffer/vulkan/opaque_fd/wgpu_buffer/dmabuf`, whose bodies have been field fills since alpha.2 (the ledger of `CHANGELOG.md:42`), `anira_capabilities_edges`/`anira_capabilities_edge`, `anira_context_probe`, the domain arms of `allocate_*` -- every declaration already froze in M2 and every enum value was pinned in M1, so this milestone changes no struct, no value and no signature. That is the proof that domains are additive under the ABI, and it is why a missing GPU runner cannot endanger the freeze: it only leaves values unconsumed on CI builds. Gate 2 does not come online here: it comes with the freeze (M6), over the `abidw` baseline taken at `v3.0.0-beta.2`, the tag that opens M6 (revised 2026-10-29; `docs/sphinx/contributing.rst`, "The ABI gates"). Effort beyond the port: 0.5 person-days.

Exit: every row of section 7's Linux tables is green against anira's Context, strict, and the prototype's `hello_inference` runs against anira instead of its own `infer/`; `v3.0.0-beta.1`.

### M5 -- plan sets, variants, the TS package, documentation (target 25.09.2026)

Folded into M2b (plan sets and variants) and M6 (the rest), 2026-10-29. One plan per (variant, candidate), `anira_handler_set_plan` over `thl::RCU` natively and the atomic on Wasm, per-plan warm-up and budget with the worst case across the set, and the stateful switch at the next boundary are M2b's PR V; the TS package rewrite (the generated `abi/`, the typed layer, ONNX Runtime Web over the JS-engine hook) went into M2 as PR 11; typedoc, the Sphinx C-API page, the JSON benchmark sweep, `[[deprecated]]` on `compat/v2.hpp` and the abidiff baseline refreshed at `beta.2` open M6, and `beta.2`, this milestone's exit tag, is now the tag at the start of M6.

### M6 -- freeze (target 25.09.2026)

M6 opens with what M5 still held (revised 2026-10-29): typedoc for the TS package; the Sphinx C-API page beside the C++ and web pages, over Doxygen on the C header as the reference; the JSON benchmark sweep with governor and wait strategy in every report; `[[deprecated]]` on every entity of `compat/v2.hpp`; the per-extension plan-report rows (`anira_plan_ext`), which the 2026-10-29 decision does not name and which travel with the rest of M5; and the abidiff baseline `abi/anira-0.<minor>.<arch>.abi` refreshed at `v3.0.0-beta.2`, the tag at the start of M6, so that the freeze diffs against a known state. That work carries its own effort beyond the freeze's (M5 was estimated at 3.0 person-days including the TS package rewrite, which PR 11 took).

Then the freeze. The freeze is one commit and the tag on it. The commit sets the registry's `abi_file_major: 3`, so that `tools/abi/gen.py` writes `abi/symbols-3.txt` and `abi/layout-3.txt` beside `abi/symbols-draft.txt`, and commits `abi/anira-3.0.<arch>.abi`; the tag `v3.0.0` turns the pair that `cmake/build-info.cmake` derives into `ANIRA_ABI_MAJOR 3`, `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR 0`, and with it `SOVERSION 3`, `MACHO_COMPATIBILITY_VERSION 3.0` and `anira-3.dll`, and `anira_check_abi` from exact match to same-major-and-newer-minor (both follow the pair; nothing is written by hand); `aniraConfigVersion.cmake` is `SameMajorVersion` already (since alpha.2); gate 2 online and gating; npm 3.0.0 on `latest`; the CHANGELOG entry "**ABI:** stable from this tag". Beside it, the browser step of the web CI, which runs the typed layer, the bundled gain configuration on the compiled-in ONNX Runtime and the ONNX Runtime Web engine in headless Chromium and Firefox (PR 9, PR 11), grows the identity model with the stale check, through the TS package on the CPU path, on both engine paths (the `onnxruntime-web` JS engine defined under `"org.anira.onnxruntime-web"`, `ONNXRUNTIME_WEB_ENGINE_ID`, and the compiled-in ORT as `ANIRA_ENGINE_ONNXRUNTIME` at `ANIRA_PROVIDER_CPU`); the browser joins section 1's table with that run, at `Host`. Effort: 0.5 person-days.

Browser WebGPU is not a v3.0.0 deliverable and moves to "After v3.0" for the reason section 6b gives -- schedule and an unmeasured admission rule, with every candidate path freeze-neutral; `anira_context_config_set_webgpu` returns `ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED` under Emscripten in 3.0. Two things ride with M6 because they cannot be added later. The headless-browser step gains a one-day spike that runs ORT-Web's WebGPU execution provider behind a GPU proxy Worker and reports end-to-end block latency and rendezvous wake latency -- the number the whole post-3.0 design turns on, and the only deliverable here that is not a design. And the thread rule of `anira_inference_thread_execute` is written into the tag table: a thread that pumps must not be a thread that calls a `_wait` twin, `anira_handler_ticket_wait`, `anira_handler_prepare` or `anira_handler_destroy`, because a pump driven from a waiting thread never delivers its own completion. The rule constrains an entry point that does freeze in 3.0.0; the asynchronous slot itself does not need reserving, because descriptor tail growth already covers it.

Exit: `v3.0.0`.

### ABI gates

| gate | name | what it checks | where it runs | online |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `anira_symbol_baseline` | the shared library's real export table (`nm -D --defined-only` / `nm -gU` / `dumpbin /exports`, read by `cmake/abi-symbols.cmake`) is exactly `abi/symbols-<major>.txt` ∪ `abi/symbols-draft.txt`: a promised or draft name that is missing fails, and so does any other exported name (a C++ symbol, an internal function, a third-party one), the linker's own symbols and, on PE, the exports the engine archives force excepted; `anira_abi_link` (`test/abi/generated/link_probe.c`, the address of every promised and draft entry) is the presence check of a static library; `anira_exports` (`test/contracts/unload`) forbids every engine runtime and every private dependency (`thl`, `moodycamel`, `nlohmann`) in the library's and a plugin-shaped module's table; an added promised name is admitted by the registry diff (`gen.py --diff-against <last tag>`: appended = minor or pre-release, removed, renamed or changed = never; advisory while the major is 0), the ABI minor being derived from the tag rather than bumped by hand | every native shared leg of `build_test`, `anira_abi_link` on every native preset; on Wasm the `-sEXPORTED_FUNCTIONS` link against `web/src/abi/exports_wasm.txt` is the presence check and the `--emit-tsd` `.d.ts` export diff in `build_web` (against `symbols-0` ∪ `symbols-draft` ∪ `symbols-emscripten`) the extras check | M2 (strict since PR 12) |
| 2 | `anira_abi_diff` | `abidiff` of `abidw` baselines `abi/anira-<major>.<minor>.<arch>.abi` with `--headers-dir` on `include/anira/abi`, `abi/abidiff-draft.supp`, `--no-added-syms`, `--fail-no-debug-info`: exit bit 8 always fails, bit 4 fails unless the minor was bumped since the last tag; a second invocation restricted to the Tier-1 headers (`tensor.h`, `processor.h`, `engine.h`, `log.h`, `status.h`) fails on bit 4 regardless | Linux x86_64 and aarch64, `RelWithDebInfo`, apt `abigail-tools`, one extra `ubuntu-24.04` job | with the freeze (M6), over the baseline taken at `v3.0.0-beta.2` |
| 3 | `anira_abi_layout` | `test/abi/generated/test_layout.c`, generated by `tools/abi/gen.py`: `_Static_assert` of every enum's width and terminator and of every Tier-1 `sizeof`/`offsetof`, plus an executable printing the table and diffing it against the committed `abi/layout-<major>.txt` (`layout-0.txt` through the pre-releases, which gen.py writes from the registry; from the freeze only a commit changing the ABI major may touch it); gen.py emits `web/src/abi/layout.ts` from the same rows | every leg natively; OBJECT target under the mobile presets; run under node in `build_web` (`anira_abi_wasm_gates`, the `wasm-abi` preset) | M1 native, M2 node |
| 4 | `anira_header_c11`, `anira_header_cxx17`, `anira_header_cxx20`, `anira_header_compat`, `anira_header_compat_with_hpp`, `anira_header_isolation` | every public header alone and the umbrellas together (`test/abi/header_c.c` over `<anira/anira.h>` and `<anira/anira_em.h>`, plus one generated TU per header) as C11 and C++17 with no anira define, under `-std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -pedantic` and `/W4 /WX /wd4201` (`test/abi/CMakeLists.txt:81`); `anira.hpp` and `compat/v2.hpp` alone and together as C++20 under the same flags; `anira_header_isolation`, strict: a positive compile with exactly the usage requirements of `anira::anira`, whose `__has_include` probes `#error` when anything private resolves (`src/`, an engine, tanh-lib, the queue, the JSON library, gtest, benchmark) | every preset: a build-driving CTest on desktop, a member of ALL on Android, iOS and WebAssembly | M1 (per file), M2 (umbrellas, strict isolation, every preset) |
| 5 | `InstallConsumer` (`test/contracts/install`), `build_install` | the installed package, shared and static: `consumer` (`anira.hpp`); `consumer_c` (pure C11, linked through the C++ driver on the static leg because anira itself is C++), the raw-C sketch of section 9 over an engine created with `anira_custom_engine_create` and `ANIRA_ENGINE_FLAG_NEEDS_NO_MODEL` in place of the candidates, with an EXPLICIT budget and a FIXED warm-up, so that it needs neither an engine runtime nor a model file, plus `anira_shutdown` and gate 4's probes on the installed include directories; `consumer_compat` (the 2.x shim); `consumer_engine` (an engine called through `anira::onnxruntime`, and the negative check that its header needs that target); `consumer_tanh_first` (a host's own shared tanh-lib linked before `anira::anira`, whose logger never sees anira's records: a shared libanira names no `thl` symbol, and in a static ELF libanira tanh-lib's symbols are local to the prelinked object); and the plugin-shaped `consumer_engine_module`, which exports no engine symbol and no `anira_*` entry | `build_install` (Linux, macOS, Windows); `consumer_tanh_first` on ELF and on a shared Mach-O libanira | M2 |
| 6 | the RTSan leg, `anira_abi_rt_contract` | at run time the whole suite under RealtimeSanitizer (`desktop-tests-rtsan`; the unload harness alone is left out, since an uninstrumented host cannot load an instrumented module); at compile time `-Werror=function-effects` PRIVATE on the consumer-shaped targets -- `anira_abi_rt_contract` (`test/abi/test_rt_contract.c`: `nonblocking` bodies calling every `[callback-safe]` tensor entry and the nonblocking Hard entries over host tensors), `anira_header_c11`, `anira_abi_link`, `consumer_c` -- and on `anira_header_cxx20` / `anira_header_compat`, never on target `anira`, where the compiler has the analysis: upstream clang 20 or later behind the GNU front end, not AppleClang, an older NDK clang or clang-cl (`test/abi/CMakeLists.txt:180-192`, `test/contracts/install/CMakeLists.txt:24-28`) | `build_sanitizer` (clang 20 via ci-actions) for the run; every leg with such a compiler for the compile | M2 |

`tools/abi/gen.py` generates from `abi/anira.yml` the headers and the umbrellas, the TypeScript mirror (`web/src/abi/enums.ts`, `layout.ts`, `bindings.ts`), the symbol lists and the Wasm export list, the layout table `abi/layout-<major>.txt` and its test, the link probe and the status-string and struct-size tables of `src/capi/generated/`, validating the header conventions of section 6a while doing so; its outputs are committed and the `anira_abi_generate` CTest (and `build_web.yml`) regenerate and diff them, so a missing Python never blocks a build. tanh-tooling stays off the critical path: `ALLOW_PREFIXES`, `EXPECT_SYMBOLS`, a Wasm branch that checks instead of skipping (`cmake/tanh/check-exports.cmake:68` skips today) and `tanh_add_abi_check()` are the items of a tanh-tooling tag after v3.0.0 that fold gates 1-2 back into the shared modules (v0.3.0 is pinned); until then gate 1 reads the export table in anira's own `cmake/abi-symbols.cmake`, and `anira_exports` passes `ALLOW_REGEX` to `tanh_add_export_check` (`cmake/tanh/check-exports.cmake:53,115`; `test/contracts/unload/CMakeLists.txt:115-121`).

### Cut order

If the schedule slips, cuts happen in this order, first cut first:

1. The abidiff job -- the symbol baselines and the layout table remain and catch every removal and every RT-layout change, on Wasm too.
2. `anira_get_proc_address` with `ANIRA_CXX_MANUAL_INIT`/`ANIRA_NO_PROTOTYPES` -- `dlopen` hosts use `dlsym` per function (taken: there is no `anira_get_proc_address`, and `anira.hpp` `#error`s on both macros).
3. `anira_model_config_to_json`/`anira_plan_report_to_json` -- the accessors suffice (half taken: `anira_plan_report_to_json` was cut with `abi/handler.h`, #173, where the report's enumerators and `anira_plan_report_find` suffice; `anira_model_config_to_json` and `anira_context_config_to_json` ship).
4. `anira_em_job_options_set_on_complete_js` -- polled tickets in the browser first.
5. The TS package rewrite beyond `abi/` and the raw exports, which the docs already bless on the RT path (revised 2026-10-29: the rewrite moved into M2 as PR 11; what is left to cut here is typedoc in M6).
6. `compat/v2.hpp` beyond `LegacyProcessor` -- the migration is documented instead (not cut: the shim shipped whole with PR 8b, #196).
7. `consumer_tanh_first` -- the link-order rule stays documented (not cut: built with PR 12, #229, on ELF and on a shared Mach-O libanira).

Never cut:

- the pinned enum values (M1);
- the frozen `anira_tensor` and the layout table (M2; `abi/layout-0.txt` through the pre-releases, `abi/layout-3.txt` from the freeze);
- `SYMBOL "anira_*"`, the C11 header compile and the symbol baselines;
- the tanh absorption and the deletion of `src/emscripten-wrappers/` (M2);
- `anira_check_abi` and the exception firewall;
- the `_wait` twins -- without them the `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING` attribute on `process` is false.

### After v3.0 (additive under ABI major 3; section 10)

Every item below lands as an appended function, enum value, extension revision or descriptor tail slot with an `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR` bump; none needs a major.

- `anira_frame` + `FrameToTensor` + `submit_frame` as `abi/draft/frame.h`, promoted when measured; `request_output`; `ANIRA_GL_SHARED_CONTEXT`.
- Typed Hard entries -- **moved into M2 on 2026-09-03 and done there: Hard contracts are not float-only, and a rule that made every v3.0.0 ring F32 would have been read as one on the model**. The host stream's element type is declared per slot on the contract -- `anira_contract_hard_set_ring_dtype(c, canonical, dtype)`, defaulting to `ANIRA_DTYPE_F32`, on `abi/config.h` since the alpha.1 tag -- and the Hard entries carry it themselves: each takes one `anira_tensor` per slot, whose dtype is checked against the slot's ring dtype (`ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG`, nothing converts), so the typed twins this item first planned (`anira_handler_process_typed` and its siblings over `void* const*`) and the float forwarders beside them were never needed, and no `_typed`, `_f32` or `_separate` entry exists (#190). The dtype belongs on the slot at `prepare` and on the tensor of the call: an input slot and an output slot are two indices of one call, their rings may hold different element types, and each tensor names its own. The tensor entries froze in `abi/handler.h` at M2 with their `_wait` twins, `[driver-thread]` and `ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`.
- The Windows, Apple and Android GPU columns, each after its own matrix run, promoting `anira_tensor_init_metal/iosurface/ahardwarebuffer/d3d12` from `abi/draft/tensor_platform.h` by moving names between baselines, never renaming.
- The deferred extension catalogue; third-party extension kinds (`anira_register_ext_kind` plus a prepare-time payload accessor, the reverse-URI prefix reserved now).
- Browser WebGPU as an explicitly asynchronous JS backend -- an `anira_engine_desc` tail slot `process_async` with a completion callback driven by `anira_inference_thread_execute` from an unblocked Worker pump, or a JSPI-linked second module variant sharing the header, with `anira_em_context_config_set_js_webgpu` reserved for the `GPUDevice` import; the Context borrows the browser's `GPUDevice` through Emscripten's WebGPU bindings instead of linking Dawn, so that device is always `ANIRA_OWNERSHIP_BORROWED` and the Dawn revision assertion of section 4 does not apply there; `WgpuBuffer` tensors are `GPUBuffer`s the page's own compute passes write; the TS package exposes `allocate_input`/`allocate_output` beside the tickets it already has for that case; and the browser joins section 1's table only after the M4 functional-probe suite has run there, like every other platform column.
- A browser video path: WebCodecs `VideoFrame` and `GPUExternalTexture` into the WebAssembly build. Neither has a representation in the header or in the TypeScript package today, so video input is absent end to end on the one platform whose decoders are already present.
- Chained `Inference` stages if a model pair demands it; a host thread-provider vtable; engine-level batching of Async jobs.
- Per-class inference-thread groups: a context that requests a scheduling class other than the default is served by its own thread group, the default group staying RealTime so 3.0.0 clients are unaffected -- `anira_context_config_set_thread_class` plus a `thread_class` key in `context.json`. This is what removes both the real-time default and the head-of-line blocking of a long job in front of a short one.
- `compat/v2.hpp` removed one minor after 3.0.
- the tanh-tooling tag that folds gates 1-2 into the shared modules (v0.3.0 is pinned today), re-pinned by anira and tanh-lib together. (The tanh-lib option `TANH_LOG_COMPILED_MAX_LEVEL` landed early, with tanh-lab/tanh-lib#37, and anira uses it.)
- `-Werror=function-effects` widened to `src/capi/` once `InferenceManager`/`Core`/`SessionElement`, `thl::core::RingBuffer` and a moodycamel wrapper are annotated.
- A Python `ctypes`/`cffi` package over `anira.h` -- the header has no function-like macro in a declaration except `ANIRA_API`/`ANIRA_CALL`/`ANIRA_NONBLOCKING`/`ANIRA_NOEXCEPT`, no bitfields, no `_Static_assert`.

### Batching, as a worked example of the tier split

Batching is not a v3.0.0 deliverable and does not compete with M1-M6 for schedule: it can land at 3.1 or 3.4 without anyone relinking. It is worth stating here because it is the first substantial feature to arrive after the freeze, and tracing it through the surface is the test of whether the tier split of section 6a was drawn in the right place.

| what batching needs | where it lives | verdict |
|---|---|---|
| declare a batch extent | `anira_tensor_spec_set_axis(s, 0, ANIRA_AXIS_BATCH, 4)` | exists; `ANIRA_AXIS_BATCH` is pinned at M1 |
| carry it at runtime | `anira_tensor.shape[]` and `ndim` | no change -- a batch axis is one shape entry, and the 216 bytes do not move |
| batched window extraction | `anira_ring_pop_windows(..., num_batches)` | exists; it is v2's `pop_samples_from_buffer(..., num_batches)` |
| batch sizes as alternatives | `anira_pipeline_add_inference(variants, ...)` with `anira_handler_set_plan` | exists; a batch size is a variant like any other |
| report which batch a plan chose | `anira_plan_info` -- Tier 2, `struct_size` first | append `uint32_t batch;` after the last v3.0.0 field, because a batch is a property of the plan and not of one slot; `ANIRA_ABI_MINOR` bump |
| an on/off or cap knob | a setter on an opaque config handle | a new function; the handle has no public layout to disturb |

Nothing lands on a Tier-1 POD, so nothing forces a major. That is the tier split doing what it was drawn for rather than luck: the batch extent rides in a frozen array that already has eight slots, the declaration rides on a handle with no layout at all, the reporting rides on a descriptor built to grow at the tail, and the selection reuses the variant machinery.

The one judgement call sits past that, at cross-ticket coalescing with a genuinely variable batch, which would relax `ANIRA_DYNAMIC`'s Time-axis-only rule (section 2). No symbol, layout or enum value changes, and no previously-working program breaks -- configurations that used to be rejected start being accepted, which is a minor with a prominent `**ABI:**` changelog entry rather than a major. The blocker there is not the ABI at all: a variable batch has no single answer to "what is the budget", and under Hard the budget is the promise.

Batching also names the gap the plan report would otherwise have had. The report is one row per plan and `budget_ms` is a field of that row, so batch-1, batch-4 and batch-16 each report their own number. A row per engine could not: a small and a large model on one engine differ in cost by construction, and with batch-1 / batch-4 / batch-16 the variants are identical *except* in cost, so a report that collapses them reports nothing. That is why the row carries `variant` beside `budget_ms` from M2 (section 6), before any variant set exists to need it.

### Open decisions, and the milestone that needs them

- **Streaming direction**: done. Streamed tensors on one side only are first-class -- section 2's legality rule, section 5's anchor that may be an output, section 7's RingChunker with independently sized sides. The v2 half shipped in v2.3.0 (#98, #99, #110: `HostConfig::k_first_streamable`, `m_tensor_is_input`, `resolve_reference()`, `test/test_OneSidedStreaming.cpp`); M2 carries it across as one `anira_ring` per streamed slot, the send rings sized by `LatencyCalculator::get_send_buffer_sizes` (`src/scheduler/LatencyCalculator.h:192`), and `test_OneSidedStreaming` runs through `compat/v2.hpp`.
- **Chunker element type**: done. Every input and output ring of the pipeline is one `thl::core::RingBuffer<T>` (`anira::RingBufferT<T>`) behind the private `anira_ring` (`src/utils/RingBuffer.h:333-342`), one arm per pinned scalar dtype -- f32, f64, f16, bf16, i8, u8, bool8, i16, i32, i64, the three without a C type stored as bits, no two dtypes sharing a ring -- chosen at `prepare` from the ring dtype the host declares for the slot on the Hard contract (`anira_contract_hard_set_ring_dtype`, `ANIRA_DTYPE_F32` when unset); the `float` instantiation of 2.x (`v2.3.0:include/anira/utils/RingBuffer.h:39`, `SessionElement::m_send_buffer` / `m_receive_buffer`) is gone. The ring is typed by the host stream and not by the model tensor -- an input ring holds what the driver pushed, an output ring holds what the driver will pop (section 7) -- and the Hard tensor entries check each host tensor's dtype against its slot's ring dtype (`ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG`, nothing converts; decision 2026-09-03: Hard is not float-only, and no dtype rule falls on the model from the entry point). The `anira_ring_*` accessors are typed rather than `float`-typed with an integer family bolted on later, because the accessor family cannot be retyped after M6: every data entry takes a `void*` plus the `anira_dtype` the caller believes it is reading, `anira_ring_dtype` reports the ring's own, and disagreement is 0 / `ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG` in `anira_handler_rt_error` with no conversion either way (section 7). What that freezes is what a processor may assume: it reads `anira_ring_dtype(r)` and never infers the ring's element type from its slot's spec.
- **Engine and provider**: done. Two independent enums, `anira_engine` and `anira_provider`, replace the packed `anira_backend`, because a provider name means the same thing across engines -- CUDA is CUDA whether ONNX Runtime or LibTorch runs it -- which the packed form hid; the pair travels as one item only as the Tier-2 `anira_backend_id`. Both are pinned at M1, and the processor context (`anira_processor_ctx`, then `anira_stage_ctx`) grew from 56 to 64 bytes for it, pre-freeze, as `abi/layout-0.txt` records. `anira_backend_id` lives in `abi/context.h` since M2, with its first consumers; M1 pinned only `ANIRA_STRUCT_BACKEND_ID = 11`.
- **Custom engines**: done. A custom engine is an object that owns its id, `anira_custom_engine_create(engine_id, &desc, &engine, err)`, added to a pipeline with `anira_pipeline_add_engine(pipe, engine, err)`, never by slot number; the id must be reverse-URI, the same reservation third-party extension kinds already make, so anira's own short engine names can never collide, and adding a second engine of one id to a pipeline is `ANIRA_ERROR_INVALID_STATE` rather than a silent replace. The object is the engine: the pool shares a prepared model between handlers that run the same object on an equal variant, whichever pipelines they come from (instances of one plugin that add one object share models as the built-in engines do; two objects never share, even under one id), so the id only resolves a model entry. Wherever the pair travels a custom engine is `ANIRA_ENGINE_CUSTOM` with its id, which is why `anira_backend_id` and `anira_plan_info` both carry `engine_id` as a string. `anira_pipeline_set_custom_backend`, `ANIRA_BACKEND_CUSTOM` and the C++ `stage::CustomBackend` are gone: a custom engine is part of the inference stage, one more implementation its candidates resolve to, and its `process` runs in `ANIRA_PHASE_INFERENCE`; in C++ it is an `anira::Engine` subclass added with `Pipeline::add_engine`, and the Emscripten twin is `anira_em_pipeline_add_js_engine(pipeline, engine_id, js_key, hook_mask, engine_flags, err)`, which passes the id the JS engine needs to find its own model row.
- **Plan addressing and selection**: done. A plan is a dense index `0..num_plans-1`, its row is `anira_plan_info` with `budget_ms` as a field, and `anira_handler_set_plan` / `anira_handler_get_plan` is the entire C selection surface -- a dense index cannot name a plan that does not exist, enumerating the set is one loop, a later dimension appends a field to the row instead of moving every signature, and the index the report hands out is the index selection takes; `anira_plan_report_find(report, &query, &plan)` (`PlanReport::find` in `anira.hpp`, `PlanReport.find` in TS) resolves a (variant, engine, provider) query to that index, `ANIRA_ERROR_NOT_FOUND` when no plan matches. M2 freezes these entries, and the table already holds one plan per model entry and candidate; `set_model(variant)` and `set_backend(engine, provider)` arrive as `anira.hpp` conveniences with the variant axis in M2b's PR V (M5 before the roadmap revision of 2026-10-29).
- **Sparse plan sets**: done. A (variant, candidate) pair whose variant carries no model entry for that candidate is not a plan and is not an error, as long as some candidate matches an entry (a pipeline whose candidates match none, or a variant left with no plan, is `ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG` at `anira_handler_create`). `ANIRA_ENGINE_FLAG_NEEDS_NO_MODEL` in `anira_engine_desc.flags` lets a model entry of that engine carry no source, neither a path nor bytes (a JSON entry without `"path"`), which is the normal shape of a passthrough or a mock; a source-less entry of any other engine is `ANIRA_ERROR_CONFIG` at `anira_handler_create`. A real engine whose model file is missing is still a failure at `prepare` (`ANIRA_ERROR_NO_SUCH_FILE`, or `ANIRA_ERROR_MODEL_LOAD` when the engine refuses the model) -- needs-no-model and model-missing are different and never collapse.
- **GPU CI hardware** for M4: which contexts run the functional-probe suite. Because the M4 surface is additive, its absence cannot break the M6 freeze -- it leaves enum values unconsumed on CI builds. Recommendation: ship the probe cache as a fixture so CPU-only CI exercises the planner against a recorded registry.
- **`ext::Entry`** (`anira_ext_entry`): chosen here (section 1b) over one extension per adapter; revisited only if a third adapter needs a different shape.
- **Anchor override on `Hard`** stays deferred until a multi-rate model exists.
- **FrameToTensor as a pipeline stage**: open, for after v3.0. Sections 1a and 7, and the post-v1 sketch of section 9, spell `FrameToTensor` as a stage ahead of the inference stage in one `Pipeline`; the pipeline built in PR 10 (#225) has `add_inference`, `add_processor` and `add_engine`, and at most one processor, which works at the host end of every slot and never crosses a domain. Whether FrameToTensor becomes a pipeline entry of its own, a second processor kind beside the one, or a form of the inference stage's input is decided with `abi/draft/frame.h`; it is post-v1 vocabulary (section 1a), so the answer moves no frozen name.

Owner decisions on the ABI, each with the recommendation:

1. **M0 tag name**: `v2.3.0` with the no-binary-promise preamble (recommended) or `v3.0.0-alpha.0`. `alpha.0` renames `on_tag.yml` artefacts, the backends-release vocabulary and every "v2 parity" reference the day before the tag, and pulls `cmake/build-info.cmake` into M0.
2. **Browser WebGPU**: out of v3.0.0 as the asynchronous backend above (recommended), or a JSPI-linked second module variant at M6, which needs an ORT wasm archive built for JSPI that the backends release does not carry.
3. **Ticket width**: `uint32_t` slot16|gen16 (recommended) or `uint64_t`. 65,535 slots and a 16-bit generation are ample for `lanes * max_in_flight + 8`, and `uint32_t` keeps `bigint` off every TS Async call.
4. **Layout identity via `ANIRA_PTR`** (recommended) or native pointers. Identity gives one `layout.ts` and one offset table on every target for one cast per producer field and the zero-the-struct rule; native pointers give 216/208 sizes and two tables.
5. **`anira_dtype` as packed `uint32_t`** (recommended) or `const anira_dtype*`. Packed keeps every signature scalar and its bytes equal `DLDataType`, so `from_dlpack` is a `memcpy`.
6. **`anira.hpp` requires C++20** (recommended); `anira.h` compiles as C11 and C++17. Designated initialisers and `std::span` are the reason; a C++17 host uses the C header.
7. **The gates-1-2 tanh-tooling tag after v3.0.0** (recommended) or bundled with M2. The M1-M3 window is the scarce resource and the anira-local scripts total about eighty lines.
8. **`ANIRA_LOG_FLAG_DISABLE_PLATFORM_SINK` default off** (recommended, v2 behaviour: the platform sink stays on beside a callback) or on, which avoids duplicate lines in DAWs that show stderr.
9. **Host thread-provider vtable**: deferred (recommended). `num_threads = 0` plus `anira_inference_thread_*` already lets a host bring its threads; a provider that spawns threads for anira needs a `[thread-safe]` spawn/join contract not yet measured.
10. **Wasm exception mode**: keep `-sNO_DISABLE_EXCEPTION_CATCHING` (recommended) or move to `-fwasm-exceptions`, which is smaller and faster but needs the ORT wasm archive built with it.
11. **Two "anira-log" drain threads** when a host also links tanh-lib: accept (recommended); a merge would make tanh-lib a shared dependency again, which is the situation being left.
12. **`anira_frame` absent from the 3.0.0 header** (recommended): values reserved now, the struct ships with `submit_frame` in `abi/draft/frame.h`; a frozen unmeasured union cannot be corrected in a minor.
13. **Unmeasured platform arms in `abi/draft/tensor_platform.h` with `symbols-draft.txt`** (recommended) or in `abi/tensor.h` outside the promise by comment. The draft folder makes the rule mechanical.
14. **GPU CI hardware for M4**: as above; recommendation: the probe cache as a fixture.
15. **Where the edge-cost policy lives**: `anira_contract_set_edge_cost` on the contract handle (recommended) or a separate handle. The contract file carries `edge_cost` and `prepare` takes one object; `anira.hpp` keeps the `edge_cost` member of `Hard`/`Async`.
16. **Third-party extension kinds post-3.0** (recommended) or `anira_register_ext_kind` plus a payload accessor in v3.0.0. Post-3.0 is additive and no external consumer exists; in-3.0 costs about a day inside the M2 week.

## TODO

- Batching: settled as an outlook, section 11 ("Batching, as a worked example of the tier split"). Every piece of it is additive inside ABI major 3, so it is out of the v3.0.0 schedule; the one open question is engine-level coalescing of Async jobs across tickets, whose blocker is the budget of a variable batch rather than anything in the ABI.
